---
title: Migrating from v0.5.x to v0.6.0
description: "Migration guide for upgrading Go AI SDK apps from v0.5.x to v0.6.0: a quick-scan table of every breaking change, one entry per change with what to search for and what to do, and a checklist for coding agents."
---

# Migrate Go AI SDK v0.5.x to v0.6.0

v0.6.0 catches up with the TypeScript AI SDK `ai@7.0.170`. Read this page if
you upgrade an application from any v0.5.x release (v0.5.0 to v0.5.3).

Most v0.5.x code compiles unchanged. A few struct fields changed type, and
those fail at compile time. The other changes match TypeScript behavior:
your code still compiles but can behave differently at run time, send
different requests, or return different error text.

How to use this page:

1. Scan the [quick-scan table](#quick-scan-table). Each row gives a pattern
   to search your code for.
2. For each pattern you find, open its section and follow **What to do**.
3. Rows without a match need no action.

Coding agents can follow the [checklist for coding agents](#for-coding-agents)
step by step.

Each entry uses the same template:

- **What changed:** the v0.5 behavior, the v0.6 behavior, and the
  TypeScript reason.
- **Am I affected?** the identifiers, fields, callbacks or wire behavior to
  search for.
- **What to do:** a before/after snippet, or "No action unless you ...".
- **Kind:** `compile error`, `silent behavior change`, `wire/protocol change`
  or `error text only`.

Entries marked "(v0.5.3)" also shipped in v0.5.3. If you are on v0.5.3
already, you can skip them.

## Quick-scan table {#quick-scan-table}

| Search for (identifier / pattern) | Change | Kind | Action | Section |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `MaxRetries:` in `GenerateObjectOptions` / `StreamObjectOptions` | `MaxRetries` is `*int`; `0` disables retries | compile error | Pass `&n`; remove `MaxRetries: 0` | [Object MaxRetries](#object-maxretries-pointer) |
| `RecordInputs:`, `RecordOutputs:` in `telemetry.Options` / `telemetry.Settings` | Fields are `*bool`; unset now records | compile error | Use `telemetry.Bool(...)`; set `false` to keep data out of spans | [Telemetry recording](#telemetry-record-pointers) |
| `EvaluateEndEvent{`, `EvaluationModelCallEndEvent{`, `EvaluateOnEndEvent{` | Embedded field renamed to the Decide type | compile error | Use `DecideStartEvent:` and similar | [Evaluate event literals](#evaluate-event-literals) |
| `acp.TextContentBlock{"` (unkeyed literal) | Type is an alias of `acp.ContentBlock` | compile error | Use keyed fields | [acp.TextContentBlock](#acp-text-content-block) |
| `==` or map keys on provider `Config` / `Provider` values, `bytedance.Error`, `klingai.Error`, `responses.OutputTextDeltaEvent`, `workflow.WorkflowResult` | Structs gained map, slice or func fields | compile error | Compare fields instead | [Non-comparable structs](#non-comparable-structs) |
| `.Text()` on a `StreamTextResult` with more than one step | Final step's text only | silent behavior change | Use `ReadAll()` or join `Steps` | [StreamText Text()](#streamtext-text-final-step) |
| `result.Output == nil`, `Output() == nil` with no `Output` option | `Output` is the final step's text | silent behavior change | Check whether you set `Output`, or read `Text` | [Output without an Output option](#output-default-text) |
| `PartialOutput()` on a `StreamText` result without `Output` | The step's text so far instead of nil | silent behavior change | Check `opts.Output` before treating it as structured | [PartialOutput default](#partial-output-default-text) |
| `OnFinish:` reading `Usage`, `ToolCalls`, `ToolResults` | Aggregated across steps | silent behavior change | Read `FinalStep` for the last step | [OnFinishEvent totals](#on-finish-aggregates) |
| `.TotalTokens` | Input + output tokens | silent behavior change | Read `Usage.Raw` for the wire value | [TotalTokens](#total-tokens) |
| `ToolResultContent.Result` in step content | The raw tool output, next to `Output` | silent behavior change | None | [Raw tool output in content](#step-content-tool-result-raw-output) |
| `ReasoningText` | Parts joined with no separator (was a newline) | silent behavior change | Join reasoning parts with a newline yourself | [ReasoningText](#reasoning-text) |
| `OnError:` in `StreamTextOptions` | Called for error parts only | silent behavior change | Handle fatal errors from `Err()` / `OnStepError` | [StreamText OnError](#streamtext-onerror) |
| `PrepareStep:` returning `Model` in `StreamTextOptions` | Override applies to one step | silent behavior change | Return the model from every call | [StreamText PrepareStep model](#streamtext-preparestep-model) |
| `OnLanguageModelCallStart:` in `StreamTextOptions` | Fires once per retry attempt | silent behavior change | Count steps with `OnStepStart` | [Model call start per attempt](#streamtext-call-start-per-attempt) |
| `OnChunk:` in `StreamTextOptions` checking `ChunkTypeError`, `ChunkTypeStart`, `ChunkTypeAbort` | Sees every error part before `OnError`, retried ones and pre-stream failures included | silent behavior change | Count failures in `OnError` | [StreamText OnChunk error parts](#streamtext-onchunk-error-parts) |
| `ExperimentalTransform` that rewrites or drops `ChunkTypeError` with `OnError` | `OnError` follows the delivered error parts | silent behavior change | Expect `OnError` for rewritten parts, none for dropped ones | [Transformed error parts](#streamtext-onerror-transformed-parts) |
| `RawFinishReason` with `StreamRetries:` | A retried step keeps only the retried attempt's value | silent behavior change | Read `FinishReason` | [Retry raw finish reason](#stream-retry-raw-finish-reason) |
| `ExperimentalTransform` + `StreamRetries` / `OnErrorRetry` | The recovered step leaves out the failed attempt | silent behavior change | Read the failed attempt from `FullStream` if needed | [Retry with a transform](#stream-retry-transform-content) |
| `OnEnd` / `OnEndEvent` / telemetry `OnError` of a multi-step `StreamText` whose later step fails to start | The call ends: `OnEnd` runs, finish reason `other`, no telemetry `OnError` | silent behavior change | Check `Err()` | [Later step failure](#streamtext-continuation-failure-end) |
| `Request()`, `.Request.Body` on `StreamText` steps or `start-step` chunks | Body only with `Include.RequestBody` | silent behavior change | Set `Include: &ai.IncludeOptions{RequestBody: true}` | [StreamText request body](#streamtext-request-body) |
| `"stream-start"`, `"response-metadata"` in `FullStream` consumers | Go-only markers left out | silent behavior change | Use `OnChunk` | [Full stream markers](#streamtext-full-stream-markers) |
| `FinishReasonOther` used to detect truncation | Truncated streams are `interrupted` | silent behavior change | Check `types.FinishReasonInterrupted` | [Truncated streams](#truncated-streams-interrupted) |
| `*ai.TimeoutError`, `errors.As(err, &te)` with `Timeout:` set | A caller cancel is `context.Canceled` | silent behavior change | Check `context.Canceled` first | [Caller cancellation](#caller-cancel-not-timeout) |
| `RepairToolCall` with `context.WithCancelCause` or `Timeout` | Returns the cancel cause or a `*TimeoutError` | silent behavior change | Match the cause / `TimeoutError` | [Repair abort reason](#repair-abort-reason) |
| `Timeout.PerStep` together with `PrepareStep:` | Step timer starts before `PrepareStep` | silent behavior change | Size `PerStep` to include it | [Step timeout start](#step-timeout-before-preparestep) |
| `MaxTokens:` set to `0` or a negative value | Rejected with `InvalidArgumentError` | silent behavior change | Leave nil or set at least 1 | [MaxTokens below 1](#max-tokens-min) |
| `OnStartEvent.Messages` with `Prompt:` | Holds the prompt as one user message | silent behavior change | Read `OnStartEvent.Prompt` for the string | [Start event messages](#start-event-messages) |
| `OnToolCallStart:` with `OnToolExecutionStart:`, `OnToolCallFinish:` with `OnToolExecutionEnd:` in `GenerateTextOptions` / `StreamTextOptions` | Only the new callback runs | silent behavior change | Move the work into the new callback | [Tool execution callback aliases](#tool-execution-callback-aliases) |
| `PrepareStep:` that sets `System` and returns its options | `System` sets the step instructions | silent behavior change | Remove a `System` assignment meant to have no effect | [PrepareStep System](#preparestep-system-override) |
| `ExperimentalStreamTranscribe` accessor errors after `FullStream().Close()`; `context.Canceled` checks | Cancel reason text after `Close`; the ctx cause after a caller cancel | silent behavior change | Match the text after `Close`, or cancel your own `ctx` | [StreamTranscribe cancel reason](#stream-transcribe-cancel-reason) |
| `StopWhen:` conditions with side effects | Run only when the loop would continue | silent behavior change | Move side effects to `OnStepFinish` | [Stop conditions](#stop-conditions-only-when-continue) |
| `"call-"` prefix checks on response, approval or UI message IDs | TS ID formats (`aitxt-`, `aiobj-`, 16 chars) | silent behavior change | Do not parse ID prefixes | [Generated IDs](#generated-id-formats) |
| `<`, `>`, `&` in expected request or stream JSON | JSON is not HTML-escaped | wire/protocol change | Update golden files | [JSON escaping](#json-not-html-escaped) |
| Custom SSE servers or test fixtures whose last event has no blank line | Unterminated final event dropped | wire/protocol change | End every event with a blank line | [SSE final event](#sse-final-event) |
| Writes to `event.Ranking` in `ai.Rerank` `OnEnd` callbacks | A copy; the result is unchanged | silent behavior change | Edit the returned result | [Rerank OnEnd ranking](#rerank-onend-ranking-copy) |
| Error of an `ai.EmbedMany` call whose parallel batches fail | The first failure in time | silent behavior change | Don't depend on the batch order of the error | [EmbedMany first failure](#embedmany-first-failure) |
| `Response.Headers` with gmicloud / zai or custom models setting only `ResponseHeaders` | Headers are filled | silent behavior change | None | [Response headers fallback](#response-headers-fallback) |
| `SetLogWarnings` with failing `StreamObject` streams | No warning log without a finish part | silent behavior change | None | [StreamObject warning logging](#streamobject-warning-logging) |
| `Prompt:` and `Messages:` both set, neither set, or `Messages: []types.Message{}` | `InvalidPromptError` | silent behavior change | Pass exactly one | [Prompt validation](#prompt-validation) |
| `RoleSystem` messages in `GenerateObjectOptions.Messages` / `StreamObjectOptions.Messages` | Rejected unless `AllowSystemMessages` | silent behavior change | Use `Instructions` | [Object system messages](#object-system-messages) |
| `*InvalidArgumentError` or "Invalid argument for parameter instructions" for `InstructionMessages` | Now `*InvalidPromptError` | silent behavior change | Match with `errors.As` | [Invalid instructions](#invalid-instructions) |
| Assistant tool calls without a tool result in `Messages` | `*types.MissingToolResultsError` | silent behavior change | Send a result for each call | [Missing tool results](#missing-tool-results) |
| JSON Schemas with `if`/`then`/`else`, `contains`, `dependencies`, `dependentSchemas` | Keywords enforced | silent behavior change | Fix inputs or schemas | [JSON Schema validator](#json-schema-validator) |
| Tool `Execute` functions that share state | Tools in a step run concurrently | silent behavior change | Lock shared state, or `ToolCallConcurrency: 1` | [Concurrent tools](#tools-concurrent) |
| `ToolChoice` unset with tools, vLLM or other OpenAI-compatible servers | Model gets `auto` | wire/protocol change | Enable auto tool choice on the server, or reset in middleware | [Unset ToolChoice](#tool-choice-auto) |
| `ToolChoice: types.ToolChoice{Type: "none"}` | Tool calls fail the call | silent behavior change | Handle `*ai.ToolChoiceViolationError` | [ToolChoice none](#tool-choice-none) |
| `OnEnd:` / `OnFinish:` treated as success after a tool choice violation | StreamText now ends with `finish` | silent behavior change | Check `Err()` | [Tool choice violation finish](#tool-choice-violation-finish) |
| A local tool named like a provider tool (`web_search`, `code_execution`) | Provider-executed calls never run locally | silent behavior change | Rename the local tool | [Provider-executed calls](#provider-executed-not-local) |
| `RoleTool` messages searched for provider tool results, `ProviderExecuted == false` | Provider results stay in the assistant message | silent behavior change | Read the assistant message | [Provider tool results](#provider-tool-results-assistant) |
| `ToolResults` entries for pending approvals or denied calls | Bookkeeping entries removed | silent behavior change | Read approval parts in `Content` | [ToolResults entries](#tool-results-bookkeeping) |
| Provider tools (`ProviderExecuted: true`, `Type: "provider"`) whose `Parameters` are not the call input; `Parameters` of `xai.MCPServer` / `xai.FileSearch` | Provider tool input validated; invalid calls marked `Invalid` and `Dynamic` | silent behavior change | Give the tool its input schema | [Provider tool input validation](#provider-tool-input-validation) |
| Tools whose `Parameters` is `*schema.OrderedSchema` or `schema.NewJSONSchema(...)` | Their calls are validated like map schemas | silent behavior change | Make the schema match the calls, or repair them | [OrderedSchema validation](#tool-input-ordered-schema-validation) |
| `ValidateUIMessages` with gateway / groq / mistral / anthropic / google / openai / xai tool outputs; reading their `OutputSchema` | TS zod output schemas | silent behavior change | Read `anyOf` | [Provider tool output schemas](#provider-tool-output-schemas) |
| `types.ToolResultContent` with `IsError` in `Content` or response messages | A `ToolErrorContent` part, replayed as `error-json` | silent behavior change | Read `ToolErrorContent` | [Provider error results](#provider-tool-error-results) |
| `StreamText` provider-executed results: `ToolResult.ToolMetadata`, UI `"toolMetadata"` | Set from the call's tool metadata | silent behavior change | None | [Provider result metadata](#stream-provider-result-tool-metadata) |
| Replayed tool error `Output.Value`, `InputSchemaInput` in `ResponseMessages`, NaN in tool outputs | TS `getErrorMessage` / `toJSONValue` / `isDeepEqualData` | silent behavior change | No action | [Response tool error replay](#response-tool-error-replay) |
| `r.Error != nil` in loops over `ToolResults` / `StaticToolResults` / `DynamicToolResults` | Failed calls are only `ToolErrorContent` parts | silent behavior change | Read `ToolErrorContent` in `Content` | [ToolResults successful only](#tool-results-exclude-failures) |
| `ContextSchema:`, `ToolApproval:` | Context and approval errors fail the call | silent behavior change | Fix config or handle `*ValidationError` | [Tool approval errors](#tool-errors-fail-call) |
| `PrepareStep` returning `ActiveTools` or `ProviderOptions` | Honored; options deep-merged | silent behavior change | Return only what the step needs | [PrepareStep tools](#preparestep-active-tools) |
| OpenAI `computer`, `local_shell`, `shell`, `apply_patch`; Anthropic `computer`, `text_editor`, `memory` tools | Client-executed | silent behavior change | Give them an `Execute` | [Client-executed tools](#client-executed-provider-tools) |
| A panic in an `AgentConfig` callback or `ErrorHandler` during `ToolLoopAgent` `Generate` / `Stream` | The per-call callback still runs; `Generate` returns its error | silent behavior change | Don't use panics to signal from callbacks | [Agent callback panics](#agent-callback-panic-isolation) |
| `.ToolCallID` used as an approval response ID (legacy `ToolLoopAgent`) | Approval IDs are generated | silent behavior change | Answer with `ApprovalID` | [Legacy approval IDs](#legacy-approval-ids) |
| Loops over legacy `StepResult.ToolResults` / `AgentResult.ToolResults` | Provider results listed first | silent behavior change | Skip `ProviderExecuted` entries | [Legacy tool results](#legacy-tool-results-provider) |
| `ToolResultOutput.Value` in legacy ToolLoopAgent step response messages | The output's JSON value, not the Go value | silent behavior change | Read `ToolResult.Result` for the Go value | [Legacy tool output JSON value](#legacy-tool-output-json-value) |
| `Request.Messages[0]` with `Include.RequestMessages` (legacy loop) | Instructions left out | silent behavior change | Read instructions from config | [Legacy request messages](#legacy-request-messages) |
| Negative `MaxRetries` on `AgentConfig` or from `PrepareCall` | Rejected | silent behavior change | Use 0 to disable | [Legacy negative MaxRetries](#legacy-negative-maxretries) |
| `errors.Is(err, context.Canceled)` for a legacy step timeout | Now `context.DeadlineExceeded` | silent behavior change | Check `DeadlineExceeded` | [Legacy step timeout error](#legacy-step-timeout-deadline) |
| `Timeout.PerStep` on the legacy agent with slow tools | Step timeout covers tools | silent behavior change | Raise `PerStep` or use `Total` | [Legacy step timeout and tools](#legacy-step-timeout-tools) |
| `ActiveTools: []string{}` on the legacy agent | No tools active | silent behavior change | Use nil for all tools | [Legacy empty ActiveTools](#legacy-empty-active-tools) |
| `PrepareCall:` that sets `Tools`, `ExperimentalToolCallers`, `ToolCallConcurrency` or `Output`; `ParseCompleteOutput` reading `Usage` | Legacy loop uses the prepared values; the parse gets the last step's usage | silent behavior change | Read `AgentResult.Usage` for the total | [Legacy PrepareCall](#legacy-preparecall-tools-output) |
| `.CallID` on `AgentResult.Steps[i]` / `WorkflowResult.Steps[i]` | Set to the run's call ID | silent behavior change | None unless you relied on it being empty | [Legacy step call ID](#legacy-step-call-id) |
| Provider-executed dynamic call of an unknown tool in the legacy agent loop, `tool not found:` | No error result, no `OnToolError` | silent behavior change | No action unless you matched the error | [Agent provider calls](#agent-provider-dynamic-unknown-tool) |
| "Unauthenticated request to AI Gateway" text, gateway auth errors from `GenerateAgent` or in telemetry `OnStepError` | Wrapped TS error | error text only | Match `*GatewayAuthenticationError` | [Gateway auth error](#gateway-auth-error) |
| `.ToolCallID` used as an approval response ID (`WorkflowAgent`) | IDs are `approval-` + call ID | silent behavior change | Answer with `ApprovalID` | [WorkflowAgent approval IDs](#workflow-approval-ids) |
| `.Result` on `ResponseMessages[0]` after an approval resume | Use `Output` | silent behavior change | Read `Output` | [Resumed approval output](#workflow-resumed-output) |
| `workflow.LanguageModelCallOptions` hooks that change a value for one step | Overrides carry over | silent behavior change | Reset the value on later steps | [WorkflowAgent PrepareStep](#workflow-preparestep-carryover) |
| `OnEnd:` / `OnError:` on `WorkflowAgent.Stream` | Failed run fires `OnEnd` after `OnError` | silent behavior change | Check the outcome in `OnEnd` | [WorkflowAgent Stream failures](#workflow-stream-failed-onend) |
| `OnEnd:` usage or `.Usage()` on `WorkflowAgent.Stream` | Input + output summed over steps; counts always set | silent behavior change | Check for 0, not nil | [WorkflowAgent Stream usage](#workflow-stream-usage) |
| `ai.workflowAgent.stream` root span of `Stream` runs | Ends with the run, with usage, errors and aborts | wire/protocol change | Expect the full run duration | [WorkflowAgent Stream root span](#workflow-stream-root-span-end) |
| `WorkflowAgent.Stream` without `StopWhen` | No default step limit | silent behavior change | Set `StopWhen` | [WorkflowAgent Stream limit](#workflow-stream-no-step-limit) |
| `ai.workflowAgent.generate` in queries or dashboards | Root span is `ai.workflowAgent.stream` | wire/protocol change | Update queries | [WorkflowAgent root span](#workflow-root-span) |
| Provider-executed call without a result in `WorkflowAgent` | Empty text tool result + warning; the run still ends | silent behavior change | No action unless you check for missing outputs | [Missing provider result](#workflow-missing-provider-result) |
| SSE event named `done`, `OnChatSendMessage:` on `WorkflowRunMultiplexer` | Ends on a `finish` chunk; sees successful sends only | wire/protocol change | Emit a `finish` chunk | [WorkflowRunMultiplexer](#workflow-multiplexer-finish) |
| `TelemetryToolCallStartEvent` / `FinishEvent.ToolContext` for `WorkflowAgent` approval-resumed tools | The filtered tool context, not nil | silent behavior change | None | [Resumed tool telemetry context](#workflow-resumed-tool-telemetry-context) |
| `CreateUIMessageStreamWithOptions`, `ToUIMessageStream` with `OnEnd` / `OnStepEnd` | Out-of-sequence chunk ends the stream | silent behavior change | Emit start chunks first; handle `*ai.UIMessageStreamError` | [UI stream sequence](#ui-out-of-sequence) |
| `KeepAliveMs` | Zero, negative or too large is an error | silent behavior change | Leave nil to disable | [KeepAliveMs](#keep-alive-range) |
| Chunks you write with a `title` on `tool-output-*`, `"finishReason": null`, an empty reasoning `id`; `part["input"] == nil` | Message state follows TS | silent behavior change | Check for a missing `input` key | [UI message state](#ui-message-state-edge-cases) |
| `ToUIMessageStream` `OnEnd`/`OnFinish` reading `e["finishReason"]` with `SendFinish: false` or a failed stream | Empty unless a `finish` chunk was sent | silent behavior change | Read the reason from `StreamTextResult` | [UI OnEnd finish reason](#ui-on-end-finish-reason) |
| Counting `"type":"error"` chunks from `StreamTextResult.ToUIMessageStream` / `CreateUIMessageStream` | One error chunk for a run that ends with an error part | wire/protocol change | Expect one | [UI single error chunk](#ui-single-error-chunk) |
| `"type":"finish"` / `"type":"abort"` chunks from a UI message stream | No `finish` without a finish part; one `abort` chunk | wire/protocol change | Treat `error` and `abort` as terminal | [UI finish and abort](#ui-finish-and-abort-chunks) |
| UI `"type":"file"` / `"reasoning-file"` chunks | Include the file's own `providerMetadata` | wire/protocol change | None, or accept the field | [UI file metadata](#ui-file-provider-metadata) |
| `ai.ToUIMessageStream` on parts that start with `ChunkTypeStart`; `NewStreamTextResultFromParts` `Stream()` / `FullStream()` | One UI chunk per part, no added framing; external streams begin with start | wire/protocol change | Send finish-step/finish parts yourself; skip a leading start | [Start-led UI streams](#ui-start-part-one-to-one) |
| UI `"type":"custom"` / `"tool-approval-request"` chunks; `ai.ToUIMessageChunk` with start or finish parts | custom keeps `providerMetadata`; equal `inputSchemaInput` omitted; start / finish map to `start` / `finish` | wire/protocol change | Use `ChunkTypeFinishStep` for the step end | [UI custom chunks](#ui-custom-chunk-metadata) |
| UI `"tool-approval-request"` / `"tool-approval-response"` chunks for automatically approved tool calls | Sent once, not twice | wire/protocol change | None | [Auto-approval chunks](#ui-auto-approval-no-duplicate) |
| `Integrations: []telemetry.TelemetryIntegration{}` | Empty slice disables telemetry | silent behavior change | Leave nil for globals | [Empty Integrations](#empty-integrations) |
| `"ai-sdk"`, `"go-ai"` tracer or instrumentation scope names, `telemetry.TracerName` | Tracers are `ai` and `gen_ai` | wire/protocol change | Update filters | [Tracer names](#tracer-names) |
| `EnrichSpan:`, runtime context objects in spans | TS attribute rules | wire/protocol change | Read flattened keys | [Span attributes](#enrich-span-attributes) |
| `experimental_onEvaluate`, `ai.evaluate`, `ai.evaluation.`, `gen_ai.operation.name=evaluate` | Renamed to decide | wire/protocol change | Update names | [Decide telemetry names](#decide-telemetry-names) |
| Telemetry integration `OnAbort` for a cancelled `GenerateText` | Integrations get `OnError` | silent behavior change | Handle `OnError` | [GenerateText abort telemetry](#generate-cancel-telemetry) |
| `OnStepStart:` / `OnStepStartEvent:` reading `e.Tools` or `e.Output` | The call's tools; `Output` is set | silent behavior change | Read `e.ActiveTools`, or `LanguageModelCallStartEvent.Tools` | [Step-start tools](#step-start-call-tools) |
| Telemetry integration `OnStepError` for `GenerateText` or `StreamText` | Fires for every failed started step, also mid-stream | silent behavior change | Don't assume the model call failed to open | [Telemetry step errors](#telemetry-step-error) |
| Telemetry integration `OnStepEnd` / `OnFinish` / `OnToolExecutionEnd` (and WorkflowAgent root `OnError` / `OnAbort`) ordered against the matching callbacks | Callbacks run first | silent behavior change | Do last-step work in the integration | [Callback and telemetry order](#telemetry-callback-order) |
| Integrations implementing the deprecated `OnStepFinish` | Runs after every integration's `OnStepEnd` | silent behavior change | Implement `OnStepEnd` | [Step finish fan-out](#telemetry-step-finish-fanout) |
| `strings.Contains(err.Error(), "failed at step"` and other prefixes | Errors returned as is | error text only | Use `errors.As` / `errors.Is` | [Unprefixed errors](#errors-unprefixed) |
| Error text with `%q`-quoted names (tool callers, tool search, decide, ID generator, download URL hostname) | TS quoting | error text only | Use `errors.As` | [Name quoting](#error-name-quoting) |
| `"no such evaluationModel"` text checks on `ExperimentalEvaluate` / `ExperimentalDecide` errors | `*providererrors.NoSuchModelError` | error text only | Use `errors.As` | [Unknown decision model](#decide-unknown-model) |
| `*registry.NoSuchProviderError` after `ResolveVideoModel` / `ResolveDecisionModel`; nil models from `registry.Resolve*` | `*providererrors.NoSuchModelError` with the full ID; unknown providers also match `NoSuchModelError` | silent behavior change | Check `NoSuchProviderError` first | [Registry errors](#registry-resolution-errors) |
| `ProviderError.Message` for an empty or non-JSON error body | HTTP status text | error text only | Read `StatusCode` / `ResponseBody` | [Non-JSON error bodies](#non-json-error-body) |
| `NoObjectGeneratedError.Cause.(*json.SyntaxError)`, `RepairText` error type | The cause is a `*providererrors.JSONParseError` | silent behavior change | Use `errors.As` / `providererrors.IsJSONParseError` | [JSONParseError cause](#object-parse-json-parse-error) |
| `.Provider()` compared with `"groq"`, `"openai"`; `ai.model.provider` | TS `config.provider` IDs (`groq.chat`) | silent behavior change | Compare prefixes | [Provider IDs](#provider-ids) |
| Custom or proxy endpoints that return non-standard JSON | "Invalid JSON response" | silent behavior change | Return the provider's schema | [Response validation](#response-validation) |
| `ProviderOptions:` with wrong value types or unknown values | `*InvalidArgumentError` | silent behavior change | Fix the options | [Provider options validation](#provider-options-validation) |
| Index-based reads of Anthropic, Bedrock or Google `result.Content` | Response order | silent behavior change | Switch on the part type | [Content order](#result-content-order) |
| `promptCacheOptions` in OpenAI Responses provider options | Validated | silent behavior change | Use documented values | [OpenAI promptCacheOptions](#openai-prompt-cache-options) |
| OpenAI chat streams handling an `error` part before output | The call fails | silent behavior change | Handle the call error | [OpenAI chat stream errors](#openai-chat-stream-error) |
| `OPENAI_API_KEY` only, with Baseten, Cerebras, DeepInfra, Fireworks, Together, Vercel, Vertex MaaS, Azure | No fallback | silent behavior change | Set the provider's own variable | [Wrapper API keys](#wrapper-api-keys) |
| `*providererrors.DownloadError` from BFL, Luma or fal | `*providererrors.ProviderError` | silent behavior change | Match `ProviderError` | [Media download errors](#media-download-errors) |
| Errors from `klingai` `VideoModel(` | Unknown IDs fail at `DoStart` | silent behavior change | Check the call error | [KlingAI VideoModel](#klingai-video-model) |
| Perplexity embedding `dimensions` / `encodingFormat` | Validated | silent behavior change | Pass valid values | [Perplexity embeddings](#perplexity-embedding-options) |
| MCP OAuth client information with a client secret and no `Issuer` | Rejected | silent behavior change | Set `Issuer` | [MCP OAuth issuer](#mcp-oauth-issuer) |
| `mcp.MCPClientConfig` without `RequestTimeoutMS`, `TimeoutMS` on MCP transports | No default request timeout | silent behavior change | Set a timeout or use ctx | [MCP timeouts](#mcp-no-default-timeout) |
| Code mode scripts that run longer than 10 s of CPU | `*codemode.CPUTimeoutError` | silent behavior change | Raise `CPUTimeoutMs` | [Code mode CPU limit](#codemode-cpu-limit) |
| Many concurrent code mode runs | `*codemode.ConcurrencyError` | silent behavior change | `codemode.SetMaxWorkers(n)` | [Code mode workers](#codemode-max-workers) |
| `github.com/tetratelabs/wazero` in your `go.mod` | v1.12.0 | silent behavior change | Move to v1.12.0 | [wazero](#wazero-version) |
| Persisted ACP lifecycle state (Cursor, GitHub Copilot, Grok Build, fx) | Does not resume | silent behavior change | Start fresh sessions | [ACP state](#acp-state) |

| `WorkflowAgent.PrepareCall` input settings | The agent's, per-call ones applied after | silent behavior change | No action unless the hook read per-call values | [PrepareCall agent settings](#workflow-preparecall-agent-settings) |

| Empty text parts in prompts | Removed before the model call (assistant parts with ProviderOptions kept) | wire/protocol change | None | [Empty text parts are removed from the prompt](#prompt-empty-text-parts) |

| Mistral Large 4 with `Reasoning` | low / medium / high | wire/protocol change | None | [Mistral Large 4 maps reasoning to low/medium/high](#mistral-large-4-reasoning) |
| Open Responses streamed step response | Server response id, model and timestamp | silent behavior change | None | [Open Responses streams report the server response](#openresponses-stream-response-metadata) |
| OpenAI-compatible raw `verbosity` | Sent | wire/protocol change | Remove it or use `textVerbosity` | [A raw verbosity option reaches OpenAI-compatible hosts](#openai-compatible-raw-verbosity) |

| Root `ai.prompt` attribute | String content for a string prompt | silent behavior change | Update parsers | [Legacy root ai.prompt keeps a string prompt as the user message](#legacy-root-prompt-string) |
| `ai.response.object` | Model key order | silent behavior change | None | [Legacy ai.response.object keeps the model key order](#legacy-response-object-order) |
| `ai.toolCall.durationMs` | Removed | silent behavior change | Use the span duration | [No ai.toolCall.durationMs on legacy tool spans](#legacy-tool-span-duration) |
| `ai.settings.maxRetries` on audio spans | Not recorded | silent behavior change | None | [No ai.settings attributes on legacy speech and transcription spans](#legacy-audio-settings) |
| Struct-literal `WorkflowAgent` without `StopWhen` | Stream has no default limit | silent behavior change | Set `StopWhen` | [Struct-literal WorkflowAgent stream has no 20-step default](#workflow-literal-stream-steps) |
| `NormalizeToolModelOutput` | FileContentBlock for file and image-data parts | silent behavior change | Handle file blocks | [Harness file parts stay file parts](#harness-tool-output-file-part) |
| `MintBridgeToken`, `BRIDGE_CHANNEL_TOKEN`, `bridge.token` | Public-key bridge auth | wire/protocol change | Drop the token; new sessions | [Harness bridges authenticate with a per-session public key](#bridge-public-key-auth) |
| MCP `elicitation/create` | TS schema and messages | silent behavior change | Send `requestedSchema` | [MCP elicitation params follow the TS schema](#mcp-elicitation-schema) |
| MCP `transport error` texts | Unwrapped TS errors | error text only | Use `errors.As` | [MCP send errors are unwrapped](#mcp-send-errors) |
| MCP HTTP discovery failures | No initialize fallback on network errors | silent behavior change | None | [MCP does not fall back to initialize after a discover network error](#mcp-discover-network-error) |
| MiniMax `video generation aborted` | TS errors | silent behavior change | Use `errors.Is` | [MiniMax video errors follow TS](#minimax-video-errors) |
| `toolwebfetch` offsets, `toolbash` truncation | UTF-16 lengths | silent behavior change | None | [toolwebfetch and toolbash count UTF-16 code units](#utf16-lengths) |
| `cerebras` / `deepinfra` media factories | NoSuchModelError | silent behavior change | Remove the calls | [Cerebras and DeepInfra unsupported model types return NoSuchModelError](#cerebras-deepinfra-model-types) |
| MCP / Vercel / Topaz / DeepInfra / OpenCode / Codex / Prodia details | TS details | wire/protocol change | None | [MCP, Vercel, Topaz, DeepInfra, OpenCode, Codex and Prodia details follow TS](#tools-r91-details) |
| `amazon-bedrock request failed` | Retryable `Cannot connect to API` ProviderError | error text only | Match on the type | [Bedrock connection failures are the retryable Cannot connect to API ProviderError](#bedrock-transport-errors) |
| `anthropic-beta` in `Config.Headers` with `DoStartBatch` | Not sent on batch start | wire/protocol change | Use `anthropicBeta` options | [Anthropic DoStartBatch drops a configured anthropic-beta header](#anthropic-batch-config-beta) |
| ElevenLabs `ExperimentalStreamSpeech` warning | TS name | error text only | Match the new text | [ElevenLabs streaming-only warning names experimental_streamSpeech](#elevenlabs-streaming-warning-text) |
| AWS credential error texts | TS / aws4fetch texts | error text only | Match the new text | [AWS credential errors use the TS texts](#aws-sigv4-credentials-text) |
| Typed values in `ProviderOptions` | Honored like the map form | silent behavior change | Check applied options | [Go-typed provider option literals are honored](#typed-provider-option-literals) |
| `MaxInputBytesPerCall` on compatible wrappers | 0 (no byte split) | silent behavior change | None | [OpenAI-compatible embedding batches are not split by bytes](#compat-embedding-byte-budget) |
| Moonshot / DeepSeek `Content` by index | Reasoning, tool calls, text | silent behavior change | Look up by type | [Moonshot and DeepSeek content order is reasoning, tool calls, text](#moonshot-deepseek-content-order) |
| `Prompt{Text:` on Responses-style models | Sent as a user message | wire/protocol change | None | [Prompt.Text is one user message for Responses-style models](#responses-prompt-text) |
| `Fireworks async ... failed` | ProviderError with status text | error text only | Use `errors.As` | [Fireworks async image errors are ProviderErrors with the status text](#fireworks-async-image-errors) |
| Open Responses with both option namespaces | Provider key wins | silent behavior change | Pass one | [Open Responses provider key wins over the openai alias](#openresponses-provider-key) |
| `provider error (0)` on cancel | Abort error returned as is | error text only | Use `errors.Is` | [Aborted calls return the abort error, not a status-0 ProviderError](#transport-errors-unchanged) |
| OpenAI / Azure / xAI / Moonshot / DeepSeek / Open Responses details | TS texts and wire details | wire/protocol change | None | [OpenAI, Azure, xAI, Moonshot, DeepSeek and Open Responses details follow TS](#provb-r91-details) |
| `bytedance error (code` | TS message | error text only | Match `Code` | [ByteDance HTTP error text is the TS message](#bytedance-error-text) |
| `baseten` media factories | NoSuchModelError | silent behavior change | Remove the calls | [Baseten media models return NoSuchModelError](#baseten-media-no-such-model) |
| BFL / AssemblyAI / fal / Gladia errors | TS error types and texts | error text only | Use `errors.As` | [BFL, AssemblyAI, fal and Gladia errors follow TS](#provc-r91-media-errors) |
| ByteDance `usage` metadata | Absent when not sent | silent behavior change | Check presence | [ByteDance usage metadata and status poll key](#bytedance-metadata-poll) |
| Repeated response headers (media providers) | Joined with ", " | silent behavior change | Split if needed | [Media providers join repeated response headers](#media-response-headers-joined) |
| BFL video polling | X-Key sent on polls | wire/protocol change | None | [BFL video polls send the X-Key credential](#bfl-video-poll-auth) |
| `.DecisionModel(` on `google.Provider` | Native Decisions API | wire/protocol change | Use `ModelGeminiDecision1` | [Google decision models use the native Gemini Decisions API](#google-decision-native) |
| `InputDetails.TextTokens` (Interactions) | Not set | silent behavior change | Read `Usage.Raw` | [Google Interactions usage has no per-modality input details](#google-interactions-usage-details) |
| `background: true` Interactions streams | GET errors in the stream | silent behavior change | Read stream errors | [Google Interactions background streams report GET failures in the stream](#google-interactions-background-get) |
| Missing-key errors (Mistral / Groq / Cohere / Perplexity media) | LoadAPIKeyError unwrapped | error text only | Use `errors.As` | [Missing API key returns LoadAPIKeyError unwrapped](#load-api-key-error-unwrapped) |
| Google / Vertex transport error texts | `Cannot connect to API` | error text only | Use `errors.As` | [Google and Vertex transport failures read Cannot connect to API](#google-vertex-connect-errors) |
| Gateway `RequestCounts` | Nil when inconsistent | silent behavior change | Handle nil | [Gateway batch status omits inconsistent request counts](#gateway-batch-request-counts) |
| Gateway `Invalid error response format` | TS text, real status | error text only | Match the new text | [Gateway invalid success bodies give the TS error text](#gateway-success-schema-errors) |
| Null tool inputs to Gemini | `args: null` | wire/protocol change | None | [Gemini sends args: null for a JSON null tool input](#gemini-null-tool-args) |
| `googlevertex/xai` headers and options | Provider headers win; options validated | wire/protocol change | Fix option types | [Vertex xAI provider headers win and compatible options are validated](#vertex-xai-headers-validation) |
| Google stream source parts | Document sources too | silent behavior change | Handle document sources | [Gemini streams File Search and RAG document sources](#gemini-stream-document-sources) |
| `advisor_20260301` inputs | Extra keys invalid | silent behavior change | None | [Anthropic advisor tool input is strict](#advisor-input-strict) |
| `ExperimentalToolCallers:` nil entries | Tool unaffected | silent behavior change | Use an empty slice to hide | [An unset tool-caller entry leaves the tool unaffected](#tool-callers-unset-entry) |
| Media download error messages | Server reason phrase | error text only | Match the new text | [Media download errors keep the server reason phrase](#download-status-reason-phrase) |
| Numeric decision question / option IDs | JS key order | wire/protocol change | None | [Decision question and option keys follow JS key order](#decision-js-key-order) |
| `NormalizeStreamProviderError` | TS message and type rules | silent behavior change | None | [Stream provider error payloads without a string message are kept](#stream-provider-error-normalization) |
| `logitBias` keys, integer and URL options | JS / zod rules | silent behavior change | None | [Provider option number keys, integers and URLs follow JS and zod](#option-schema-js-coercion) |
| `MockTextStream` `Read` | io.Reader contract | silent behavior change | None | [testutil.MockTextStream.Read follows io.Reader](#mock-text-stream-read) |
| Rev.ai errors and `config` field | TS codes, headers and key order | wire/protocol change | None | [Rev.ai job errors, headers and config order follow TS](#revai-r91-details) |
| `ElementStream`, `ElementStreamWithOutput` | First text block only; restarted by stream retries | silent behavior change | None | [Element streams follow the TS output stage](#element-stream-first-block) |
| `PrepareStep` / tool message `ProviderOptions` with nil values | nil replaces the base value | silent behavior change | Leave the key out | [A nil provider option override replaces the base value](#provider-options-null-override) |
| MCP events / input_required, MiniMax video usage, Topaz video key and resolution, Vercel runtime + image, DeepInfra image missing key | TS wire order, error types and checks | wire/protocol change | None | [MCP, MiniMax, Topaz, Vercel Sandbox and DeepInfra details follow TS (round 92)](#tools-r92-details) |
| WorkflowAgent stream `OnStepEnd` | Not called for a step that pauses on a client tool or approval | silent behavior change | Use telemetry or OnEnd | [WorkflowAgent stream skips OnStepEnd for a paused step](#workflow-stream-paused-step-end) |
| `telemetry.GetBaseAttributes` | Skips credential headers in any case | silent behavior change | None | [GetBaseAttributes skips credential headers in any case](#telemetry-base-attributes-credential-headers) |
| `ai.response.toolCalls`, `ai.toolCall.args`, `gen_ai.tool.call.arguments` | Model key order | silent behavior change | Compare parsed JSON | [Telemetry tool-call inputs keep the model's key order](#telemetry-tool-input-key-order) |
| `TelemetryFinishEvent.ProviderMetadata` of speech / transcribe; `ai.response.providerMetadata` `"{}"` | The model's own value; nil when none | silent behavior change | Treat nil as none | [Speech and transcription end events carry the model's provider metadata](#audio-end-event-provider-metadata) |
| Transport connect / response-header timeouts; `*DownloadError` from a media poll or download network failure | Retryable `Cannot connect to API` error, retried; not `context.DeadlineExceeded` | silent behavior change | Use `errors.As(err, &netErr)` | [Transport timeouts are the retryable Cannot connect to API error](#transport-timeouts-retryable) |
| `retry-after`, `retry-after-ms` response headers | JS `parseFloat` and `Date.parse` | silent behavior change | None | [Retry-After headers are read like TS](#retry-after-js-parsing) |
| `AWS_BEARER_TOKEN_BEDROCK` set after `bedrock.New` | Read once at provider creation | silent behavior change | Create the provider after setting it | [Bedrock reads AWS_BEARER_TOKEN_BEDROCK when the provider is created](#aws-api-key-env-at-creation) |
| `ResponseMetadata.Timestamp` from Google Interactions, Gateway, Bedrock; Anthropic Files times; Google realtime `ExpiresAt` | Date.parse, millisecond precision | silent behavior change | None | [Provider timestamps are parsed like JS Date](#js-date-timestamps) |
| BFL `webhookUrl`, `polling_url`; Perplexity citations | zod `z.url()` check and output | wire/protocol change | None | [BFL and Perplexity URL values follow zod z.url()](#zod-url-values) |
| Harness turns with `Output`: `PartialOutput()`, text deltas | First text block, grouped deltas | silent behavior change | None | [Harness turns with an output follow the TS output stage](#harness-output-stage) |
| `ElementStream` / `ElementStreamWithOutput` with `Stream()`, `FullStream()` or `Chunks()` on the same result | Own replaying cursor (not the shared `Stream()`); harness results follow the turn's first text block | silent behavior change | None | [Element streams read their own stream cursor](#element-stream-own-cursor) |
| gateway.serviceTier with another provider's serviceTier; empty gateway `ProviderOptions` | Other provider's tier option kept in the body; `{}` sent | wire/protocol change | None | [Gateway forwards provider options as given](#gateway-provider-options-as-given) |
| Vertex transcription, Cloud TTS, embedding, video `ProviderError.Data` | No details; message read even with a non-array details | error text only | None | [Vertex errors follow the Vertex error schema](#vertex-error-schema) |
| Deepgram transcription with `results.channels: []` or no alternative | The call fails with the TS TypeError text | silent behavior change | Handle the error | [Deepgram transcription without a channel or alternative fails](#deepgram-empty-channels-error) |
| `ExperimentalStreamTranscribe` `Responses()[i].Body`; `TranscriptionStreamOptions.ProviderOptions` | No body; `{}` when unset | silent behavior change | Use `IncludeRawChunks` | [Streaming transcription responses carry no body](#stream-transcribe-response-body) |
| `FullStream()` of `ExperimentalStreamTranscribe` / `ExperimentalStreamTranslate`, then an accessor before reading | Accessor waits for the reader | silent behavior change | Read `FullStream` to EOF first | [Streaming audio results wait for the FullStream reader](#stream-audio-fullstream-demand) |
| `Transcribe` with an invalid `AudioBase64`, error text | TS InvalidDataContentError text | error text only | Use `errors.As` | [Transcribe reports undecodable base64 audio with the TS text](#transcribe-base64-error-text) |
| `VideoPromptImage.DataString` holding an `http(s)://` URL | Sent as a URL file | silent behavior change | None | [Video image data strings with an http(s) URL are sent as URLs](#video-datastring-url) |
| `RetryError` text with an apostrophe | Quotes not escaped | error text only | Match the plain text | [The non-retryable RetryError message keeps quotes as is](#retry-non-retryable-message-quotes) |
| Provider, MCP server or sandbox API responses with differently cased keys | Key ignored (exact match, as zod) | silent behavior change | Send the documented keys | [Provider responses are decoded with exact keys](#provider-response-keys-case-exact) |
| Gateway decision answers, Gateway model `Warnings`; non-object `providerOptions.gateway` on decisions | Only the type's own fields; the call fails | silent behavior change | None | [Gateway decision answers and warnings keep only their own fields](#gateway-response-schema-keys) |
| Google Files upload without `file`, Google / Vertex "No videos in response", Interactions file URIs | TS error texts, WHATWG URLs | error text only | None | [Google and Vertex error texts for malformed responses follow TS](#provg-r94-error-texts) |
| Interactions `systemInstruction: ""`, `previousInteractionId: ""`, empty enum options | Sent; enum options fail | wire/protocol change | Leave them out | [Google Interactions sends empty string options](#google-interactions-empty-options) |
| Custom `BaseURL` of realtime, live and streaming WebSocket models | WHATWG serialization; Invalid URL | wire/protocol change | None | [Realtime and live WebSocket URLs follow the WHATWG URL parser](#websocket-urls-whatwg) |
| `context.WithCancelCause` with `ExperimentalStartBatch`, tool refinement / queued tools, `WorkflowAgent`, `StreamText`, limited tool concurrency, polling, WebSocket streams, MCP transports | The cause, not `context.Canceled` | silent behavior change | Check `errors.Is(err, cause)` | [Cancellations with a cause return the cause](#abort-cause-r94) |
| Context cancelled during a retry backoff | `context.Canceled` / `DeadlineExceeded`, not the cause or the v0.5 text | error text only | Use `errors.Is(err, context.Canceled)` | [A cancel during a retry backoff returns the context error](#retry-abort-during-backoff) |
| Open Responses `FinishReason` with tool calls | `length` / `content-filter` / `other` like TS | silent behavior change | Handle those reasons | [Open Responses maps the finish reason like TS](#openresponses-finish-reason-ts) |
| OpenAI Responses options set to `""`; `""` reasons, ids, namespaces in OpenAI / xAI / Open Responses results | Sent and kept | wire/protocol change | Leave unset options out | [OpenAI, xAI and Open Responses keep present empty strings](#provb-empty-string-values) |
| xAI video `null` options; malformed DeepSeek Responses / OpenAI batch bodies | Rejected like the TS schemas | silent behavior change | Leave unset options out | [OpenAI, xAI and DeepSeek validate more responses and options](#provb-r95-validation) |
| `ProviderMetadata["google"]["interactionId"]` on Interactions built-in tool calls | No provider metadata | silent behavior change | Read the response metadata | [Google Interactions built-in tool calls carry no provider metadata](#interactions-builtin-tool-call-metadata) |
| Interactions `imageConfig`, `agentConfig`, `responseModalities`, allowlist `transform` options | Checked like the TS schema | silent behavior change | Use the documented values | [Google Interactions options follow the TS option schema](#google-interactions-option-schema) |
| Dial error text of streaming WebSocket models | TS socket error text | error text only | Match the TS text | [Streams that fail to open their WebSocket report the TS socket error](#websocket-open-failure-ts-text) |
| Gateway speech stream with undecodable audio | Fails with `*speechstream.InvalidCharacterError` | silent behavior change | Use `errors.As` | [The Gateway speech stream fails on audio it cannot decode](#gateway-speech-undecodable-audio) |
| `SkillsAPI.UploadSkill` with stream file data | `UnsupportedFunctionalityError` | error text only | Pass the bytes | [Anthropic Skills uploads reject stream file data](#anthropic-skills-stream-upload) |
| `jsonstr.Marshal` with NaN / ±Inf; provider values read from `1e400` passed to `encoding/json.Marshal` | Read as ±Inf, written as `null`; `jsonstr.Marshal` no longer errors | silent behavior change | Use `jsonstr.Marshal` for provider values | [Numbers beyond the double range](#out-of-range-numbers) |
| `__proto__` or `constructor.prototype` keys in provider responses, stream chunks or model JSON | Fail with `*providererrors.JSONParseError` | silent behavior change | Remove such keys from fixtures | [JSON payloads with `__proto__` keys fail](#json-proto-payloads-fail) |
| `OnAbort:` of `workflow.WorkflowAgent` reading the steps | Full step results | silent behavior change | None | [WorkflowAgent OnAbort steps](#workflow-onabort-full-steps) |
| `mcp.SplitMCPAppTools` with a non-array tool `visibility` | String substring match; other values error | silent behavior change | Send an array | [MCP App visibility](#mcp-app-visibility-raw) |
| MCP string error codes, loose capability extras, unknown tool-result parts, SSE `endpoint` with `//host`; Vercel `ListDirectory` | Follow the TS schemas and JS semantics | silent behavior change | None | [MCP and sandbox round 96 details](#tools-r96-details) |
| OpenAI Responses `reasoningEffort: ""`, fractional number options, done function-call items; xAI `incomplete_details` | Sent as given; read verbatim | wire/protocol change | Leave unset options out | [OpenAI and xAI round 96 details](#provb-r96-details) |
| Deepgram / AssemblyAI `AudioBase64`, Deepgram response fields, Cartesia realtime events and client secrets, non-finite error codes | Follow TS | silent behavior change | None | [Media provider round 96 details](#provc-r96-details) |
| Setup send failure of ElevenLabs speech and OpenAI / Google / Vertex live transcription and translation streams | `stream-start` first, then the error | silent behavior change | Expect `stream-start` before the error | [stream-start before a setup failure](#stream-start-before-setup-failure) |
| `askUserQuestions` input with `null` optional keys; differently cased output keys | Rejected / ignored like TS | silent behavior change | Leave optional keys out | [askUserQuestions schema](#harness-questions-tool-schema) |

## Compile-time changes {#compile-time-changes}

### GenerateObject and StreamObject MaxRetries is a pointer {#object-maxretries-pointer}

- **What changed:** `GenerateObjectOptions.MaxRetries` and
  `StreamObjectOptions.MaxRetries` changed from `int` to `*int`, like
  `GenerateTextOptions.MaxRetries` and TS `maxRetries`. nil uses the default
  of 2 retries. An explicit `0` now disables retries; in v0.5, `0` selected
  the default. A negative value returns `*errors.InvalidArgumentError`.
- **Am I affected?** `MaxRetries:` in a `GenerateObjectOptions` or
  `StreamObjectOptions` literal.
- **What to do:** pass a pointer. Remove `MaxRetries: 0` if you meant the
  default.
- **Kind:** compile error

**Before:**

```go skip-compile
result, err := ai.GenerateObject(ctx, ai.GenerateObjectOptions{
    Model:      model,
    Prompt:     "Generate a recipe",
    Schema:     recipeSchema,
    MaxRetries: 5,
})
```

**After:**

```go skip-compile
retries := 5
result, err := ai.GenerateObject(ctx, ai.GenerateObjectOptions{
    Model:      model,
    Prompt:     "Generate a recipe",
    Schema:     recipeSchema,
    MaxRetries: &retries,
})
```

### Telemetry RecordInputs and RecordOutputs are pointers, and unset records {#telemetry-record-pointers}

- **What changed:** `telemetry.Options.RecordInputs` and `RecordOutputs`
  (also through the deprecated `telemetry.Settings`) changed from `bool` to
  `*bool`. nil means record, as an omitted `recordInputs` / `recordOutputs`
  does in TS. Only an explicit `false` turns recording off.
  `DefaultSettings()`, `WithRecordInputs(bool)` and `WithRecordOutputs(bool)`
  keep their signatures. `ShouldRecordInputs()` / `ShouldRecordOutputs()`
  report the effective value.
- **Am I affected?** `RecordInputs:` or `RecordOutputs:` in a literal (compile
  error), or any hand-built `telemetry.Options{}` / `telemetry.Settings{}`
  that leaves them unset (silent change). **Privacy:** in v0.5 such a literal
  recorded nothing. In v0.6 it records prompts, messages, tool arguments,
  generated text, objects and tool results in your spans.
- **What to do:** wrap values in `telemetry.Bool(...)`. Set both fields to
  `telemetry.Bool(false)` wherever spans must not contain prompts or outputs.
- **Kind:** compile error

**Before:**

```go skip-compile
tel := &telemetry.Options{
    IsEnabled:     telemetry.Bool(true),
    RecordInputs:  false,
    RecordOutputs: true,
}
```

**After:**

```go
tel := &telemetry.Options{
    IsEnabled:     telemetry.Bool(true),
    RecordInputs:  telemetry.Bool(false), // keep prompts out of spans
    RecordOutputs: telemetry.Bool(true),  // or leave unset: nil records
}
```

### Evaluate end-event struct literals use the Decide field names {#evaluate-event-literals}

- **What changed:** `experimental_evaluate` is now `experimental_decide`, as
  in TS. The old type names are deprecated aliases, so their embedded field
  is named after the new type. Reading promoted fields still compiles.
- **Am I affected?** Struct literals of these types, usually in tests:

  | Type | Embedded field to use in a struct literal |
  | --- | --- |
  | `telemetry.EvaluateEndEvent` | `DecideStartEvent` |
  | `telemetry.EvaluationModelCallEndEvent` | `DecisionModelCallStartEvent` |
  | `ai.EvaluateOnEndEvent` | `DecideOnStartEvent` |

- **What to do:** rename the embedded field in the literal, or move to the
  Decide types (see [Deprecated](#deprecated)).
- **Kind:** compile error

```go
end := telemetry.EvaluateEndEvent{DecideStartEvent: telemetry.DecideStartEvent{}}
_ = end
```

### Unkeyed acp.TextContentBlock literals {#acp-text-content-block}

- **What changed:** `acp.TextContentBlock` is now an alias of
  `acp.ContentBlock` (TS #23587), which also carries image blocks (`Data`,
  `MimeType`).
- **Am I affected?** Unkeyed literals such as
  `acp.TextContentBlock{"text", "hello"}`. Keyed literals and
  `[]TextContentBlock` fields keep working.
- **What to do:** use keyed fields.
- **Kind:** compile error

```go
block := acp.TextContentBlock{Type: "text", Text: "hello"}
```

### Some structs are no longer comparable {#non-comparable-structs}

- **What changed:** these structs gained map, slice or function fields (for
  example `Config.Headers map[string]string`), so Go no longer lets you compare them with `==` or use
  them as map keys: `Config` and `Provider` in `assemblyai`, `cartesia`,
  `deepgram`, `elevenlabs`, `fishaudio`, `gladia`, `hume`, `moonshot`,
  `replicate`, `revai` and `stability`; `cerebras.Config`;
  `bytedance.Error`; `klingai.Error`; `openai/responses.OutputTextDeltaEvent`;
  `workflow.WorkflowResult`.
- **Am I affected?** `==`, `!=`, `switch` on, or map keys of these values.
- **What to do:** compare the fields you care about, or use
  `reflect.DeepEqual` in tests.
- **Kind:** compile error

## Core text and streaming {#core-text-and-streaming}

### StreamTextResult.Text() is the final step's text {#streamtext-text-final-step}

- **What changed:** `StreamTextResult.Text()` (and the end event and
  telemetry text) returned the text of every step joined. It is now the
  final step's text, as TS `result.text`. `ReadAll()` still returns every
  step's text. The same applies to harness and external streams.
- **Am I affected?** `.Text()` on a `StreamTextResult` of a multi-step run
  (tools with `StopWhen`).
- **What to do:** use the text that `ReadAll()` returns, or join
  `Steps()` yourself:

  ```go
  all, err := res.ReadAll() // every step's text, as v0.5 Text()
  if err != nil {
  	log.Fatal(err)
  }
  _ = all
  ```

- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Output is the text when no Output option is set {#output-default-text}

- **What changed:** with no `Output` option, `GenerateTextResult.Output`
  and `StreamTextResult.Output()` were nil. They are now the final step's
  text (a `string`), as TS `output ?? text()`. `GenerateText` sets it for a
  `stop` finish (also `""`), or a finish other than `tool-calls` with text,
  and leaves it nil otherwise. `StreamText` sets it once the run completes,
  whatever the finish reason, and leaves it nil for a failed or aborted
  run. The legacy `ToolLoopAgent` (`Execute`, `GenerateAgent`) and
  `WorkflowAgent.Generate` already returned the text in `AgentResult.Output`
  and `WorkflowResult.Output`, but now follow the `GenerateText` rule: a run
  that ends on a `tool-calls` finish has a nil `Output`. The end event's
  `Output` stays nil without an `Output` option, `WorkflowStreamResult.Output()`
  stays nil without one (TS stream mode parses only an output spec), and so
  does a harness turn's result. JSON-encoded `GenerateTextResult` values now
  carry an `"output"` field.
- **Am I affected?** Code that tests `result.Output == nil` or
  `res.Output() == nil` to detect a call without an `Output` option, or that
  type-switches on `Output` without a `string` case; code that reads
  `AgentResult.Output` / `WorkflowResult.Output` after a run that hit its
  step limit on tool calls.
- **What to do:** no action unless you rely on a nil `Output`. Check whether
  you passed an `Output` option instead, and read `Text` (or `Text()`) for
  the text.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### PartialOutput is the step's text when no Output option is set {#partial-output-default-text}

- **What changed:** without an `Output` option,
  `StreamTextResult.PartialOutput()` is now the current step's text so far,
  a `string`. v0.5 returned nil. TS `streamText` parses partial output with
  `output ?? text()` whenever the run keeps its replay buffer, so
  `partialOutputStream` yields the text (review round 70). A
  `StreamTextLowMemory` run without an `Output` still returns nil (TS
  rejects `partialOutputStream` there), and so does
  `WorkflowStreamResult.PartialOutput()` without an output spec (TS
  WorkflowAgent stream mode parses only an output spec).
- **Am I affected?** `PartialOutput()` on a `StreamText` result created
  without `Output:`, especially `!= nil` checks.
- **What to do:** no action unless you use `PartialOutput()` to detect
  structured output. Check your own option instead:

  ```go
  func structuredPartial(opts ai.StreamTextOptions, result *ai.StreamTextResult) any {
  	if opts.Output == nil {
  		return nil // v0.5: PartialOutput() was nil without Output
  	}
  	return result.PartialOutput()
  }
  ```

- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### OnFinishEvent aggregates across steps {#on-finish-aggregates}

- **What changed:** `OnFinishEvent` usage, tool calls and results, sources,
  files and warnings are totals across all steps, and the event adds
  `Content`, `FinalStep` and `ResponseMessages`. v0.5 reported the last
  step.
- **Am I affected?** `OnFinish:` callbacks that read `Usage`, `ToolCalls` or
  `ToolResults` as the last step's values.
- **What to do:** read `event.FinalStep` for the last step only.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Step content tool results keep the raw tool output {#step-content-tool-result-raw-output}

- **What changed:** a `types.ToolResultContent` in `StepResult.Content`,
  `GenerateTextResult.Content`, `StreamTextResult.Content()` and the step and
  end events keeps the tool's raw output in `Result` next to the
  model-facing `Output`. v0.5 cleared `Result` whenever it set `Output`, so
  the raw value of a `ToModelOutput` tool was not in the content at all. TS
  step content `tool-result.output` is the raw output; the model output is
  built only for the response messages (review round 74). Response messages
  and the next prompt are unchanged: their tool results carry `Output` only.
- **Am I affected?** Code that reads `ToolResultContent.Result` from step or
  result content expecting nil, or that marshals step content to JSON (the
  part gains `"result"`).
- **What to do:** no action unless you relied on `Result` being nil. Read
  `Output` for what the model saw and `Result` for the tool's own value.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Usage.TotalTokens is input + output {#total-tokens}

- **What changed:** `Usage.TotalTokens` is input tokens plus output tokens
  for every provider, as TS computes it. v0.5 copied the provider's wire
  `total_tokens`, which some providers compute differently. Core sets it
  from input + output whenever either count is reported, replacing a total
  the model itself set (a custom `LanguageModel`, or a provider that passes
  its wire total through), in `GenerateText`, `GenerateObject`,
  `StreamObject`, `StreamText` and the legacy `ToolLoopAgent` loop (TS
  `asLanguageModelUsage`, whose model usage has no total of its own; review
  rounds 74-75). A total reported with neither count is kept.
- **Am I affected?** Billing or metrics code that reads `.TotalTokens`.
- **What to do:** no action unless you need the provider's own total. Read
  it from the usage's raw provider data.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### ReasoningText joins parts with no separator {#reasoning-text}

- **What changed:** `ReasoningText` joins reasoning parts with no separator,
  as TS. v0.5 put a newline between them.
- **Am I affected?** Code that splits `ReasoningText` on newlines or
  compares it with a stored value.
- **What to do:** join the step's reasoning parts with `"\n"` yourself if
  you need the v0.5 text.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### StreamText OnError sees error parts only {#streamtext-onerror}

- **What changed:** as in TS, `OnError` is called for the `error` parts of the
  stream: a provider error chunk, a model call that fails to open, a failure
  before the first stream, a later step that fails to prepare or open, an
  incomplete model stream, a tool choice violation, a tool context that fails
  its schema. When the model stream itself fails (`Next` returns an error),
  or a URL-backed generated file download, a tool input callback
  (`OnInputStart`, `OnInputDelta`, `OnInputAvailable`), an approval resolved
  inside the stream or a tool output conversion (`ToModelOutput`) fails, the
  run ends with that error without calling `OnError`. `Err()`, `ReadAll()`,
  `OnStepError` and the telemetry `OnError` still report it. A run whose
  context is cancelled before its first model stream emits `start` and an
  `abort` chunk and calls `OnAbort`, not `OnError`.
- **Am I affected?** `OnError:` in `StreamTextOptions` used as the only place
  that sees fatal stream failures.
- **What to do:** read fatal errors from `Err()` or `OnStepError`:

  ```go
  res, _ := ai.StreamText(ctx, opts)
  if _, err := res.ReadAll(); err != nil {
  	log.Printf("stream failed: %v", err) // also reported to OnStepError
  }
  ```

- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### A StreamText PrepareStep model applies to one step {#streamtext-preparestep-model}

- **What changed:** as in TS (`prepareStepResult?.model ?? model`) and
  `GenerateText`, each step starts from the call's `Model`, and `PrepareStep`
  receives it in `PrepareStepOptions.Model`. In v0.5 a model that
  `PrepareStep` returned stayed in effect for every following step, and later
  `PrepareStep` calls received it as `Model`.
- **Am I affected?** A `StreamText` `PrepareStep` that returns a `Model` only
  on some steps.
- **What to do:** to keep an override for later steps, return it from every
  `PrepareStep` call.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### StreamText fires OnLanguageModelCallStart once per attempt {#streamtext-call-start-per-attempt}

- **What changed:** as in TS, each retried attempt to open the model stream
  (`MaxRetries`) is a new model call. `OnLanguageModelCallStart` fires again,
  and the call's `ResponseTimeMs` runs from that attempt. A failed attempt
  gets no `OnLanguageModelCallEnd`.
- **Am I affected?** Code that pairs `OnLanguageModelCallStart` with
  `OnLanguageModelCallEnd`, or counts steps with call starts.
- **What to do:** key on the last start, or count steps with `OnStepStart`,
  which fires once.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### StreamText OnChunk sees every error part before OnError {#streamtext-onchunk-error-parts}

- **What changed:** as in TS, `OnChunk` is called for an `error` part before
  `OnError` (review round 69). For an error part of the model's stream it is
  called with the part as the model sent it, before `ExperimentalTransform`
  and the stream retry decision, so `OnChunk` also sees an error part that
  `StreamRetries` retries (it is not in the full stream) and one a transform
  drops; it is not called again for that part. A run that fails before its
  first model stream (an invalid prompt, a model call that fails to open)
  now passes its `start` and `error` parts to `OnChunk`, and a run cancelled
  before its first model stream passes `start`, then calls `OnAbort`, then
  passes `abort`. v0.5 called `OnError` first, never passed a retried error
  part to `OnChunk`, and passed no parts of such runs.
- **Am I affected?** `OnChunk:` callbacks in `StreamTextOptions` that check
  for `provider.ChunkTypeError`, `ChunkTypeStart` or `ChunkTypeAbort`, or
  that rely on `OnError` running first.
- **What to do:** no action unless you do that. Count failures in `OnError`,
  not in `OnChunk`:

  ```go
  func countFailures(opts *ai.StreamTextOptions, n *int) {
  	opts.OnError = func(ctx context.Context, err error) {
  		*n++ // OnChunk also sees retried error parts
  	}
  }
  ```

- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### StreamText OnError runs for the error parts the stream delivers {#streamtext-onerror-transformed-parts}

- **What changed:** with `ExperimentalTransform`, `OnError` now follows the
  error parts that leave the transform chain. An error part a transform
  replaces with a different error reaches `OnChunk` and `OnError` again with
  the new error. An error part that a transform drops from a pre-stream
  failure, a later step's failure or a failed stream retry no longer reaches
  `OnError`. A model stream's own error part still reaches `OnChunk` and
  `OnError` once, before the transforms. TS's event processor calls onChunk
  and onError for every error part except the error value the model stream
  reader already reported (review round 70).
- **Am I affected?** `ExperimentalTransform` functions that rewrite or drop
  `provider.ChunkTypeError` chunks while `OnError:` is set.
- **What to do:** no action unless your transform rewrites or drops error
  parts. To see the run's error whatever the transform does, read `Err()`
  after the stream:

  ```go
  func runError(result *ai.StreamTextResult) error {
  	return result.Err() // not affected by ExperimentalTransform
  }
  ```

- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### A retried StreamText step drops the failed attempt's raw finish reason {#stream-retry-raw-finish-reason}

- **What changed:** when `StreamRetries` retries a step whose failed attempt
  had already sent a finish part, the step's `RawFinishReason` (and
  `StreamTextResult.RawFinishReason()`) comes from the retried attempt only:
  empty if its finish part has none. v0.5 kept the failed attempt's value.
  TS resets the raw finish reason at the retry boundary (review round 69).
- **Am I affected?** Code that reads `RawFinishReason` with `StreamRetries`
  set, for a provider that sends an error after its finish part.
- **What to do:** no action unless you do that. Read the unified reason:

  ```go
  func stepFinish(step types.StepResult) types.FinishReason {
  	return step.FinishReason // RawFinishReason may be empty after a retry
  }
  ```

- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### A stream retry with a transform keeps only the recovered attempt {#stream-retry-transform-content}

- **What changed:** with `ExperimentalTransform` and `StreamRetries`, the
  step recorded after a retried model call now holds only the recovered
  attempt's transformed content: `Text()`, `Steps()`, `Content()`,
  `ResponseMessages()`, the next step's prompt, and `Output()`. v0.5 also
  kept the failed attempt's text and parts, which could break `Output`
  parsing. TS resets the recorded content at the retry boundary (review
  round 70).
- **Am I affected?** `StreamText` calls that set both
  `ExperimentalTransform:` and `StreamRetries:` / `OnErrorRetry:`.
- **What to do:** no action unless you relied on the failed attempt's text
  in the result. It is still in the full stream:

  ```go
  func failedAttemptText(result *ai.StreamTextResult) string {
  	var all strings.Builder
  	s := result.FullStream()
  	for {
  		c, err := s.Next()
  		if err != nil {
  			return all.String()
  		}
  		if c.Type == provider.ChunkTypeText {
  			all.WriteString(c.Text) // includes the failed attempt's deltas
  		}
  	}
  }
  ```

- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### A later StreamText step that fails to start ends the call {#streamtext-continuation-failure-end}

- **What changed:** when a later step of a `StreamText` call fails to
  prepare or open (a `PrepareStep` result that fails validation, a prompt
  that fails conversion, or a model call that returns an error), the stream
  now ends with that step's `error` part and no `finish` part. `OnStepError`
  runs for the step if it started, then `OnEnd` / `OnEndEvent` and the
  telemetry `OnFinish` (onEnd) run with the steps recorded so far.
  `FinishReason()` is `other` and `TotalUsage()` is empty (TS
  `recordedFinishReason ?? 'other'`, `recordedTotalUsage ?? null usage`).
  The telemetry `OnError` is no longer called. `Err()` still returns the
  error. v0.5 failed the call: telemetry `OnError`, no `OnEnd`. Also,
  `OnStepError` now runs before the telemetry `OnStepError` (TS order).
  `WorkflowAgent.Stream` is unchanged (review round 70).
- **Am I affected?** Multi-step `StreamText` calls that rely on `OnEnd` not
  running after a failed later step, on the telemetry `OnError` for it, or
  on `FinishReason()` / `TotalUsage()` after it.
- **What to do:** check `Err()` in `OnEnd`, or after the stream:

  ```go
  func endedWithError(result *ai.StreamTextResult) bool {
  	return result.Err() != nil // v0.5: OnEnd did not run after a failed later step
  }
  ```

- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### StreamText keeps the request body only when asked {#streamtext-request-body}

- **What changed:** as in TS (`include.requestBody` defaults to false), the
  step request, the `start-step` chunk and `Request()` carry the provider's
  request body only with `Include: &ai.IncludeOptions{RequestBody: true}`.
  `GenerateText` already had this default. The `GenerateText` step-end
  event's request and response bodies now follow `Include` too.
- **Am I affected?** Code that reads `Request().Body`, a step's
  `Request.Body`, or the bodies in a `GenerateText` step-end event.
- **What to do:** set `Include`:

  ```go
  opts := ai.StreamTextOptions{Include: &ai.IncludeOptions{RequestBody: true, ResponseBody: true}}
  _ = opts
  ```

- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### The StreamText full stream drops the Go-only markers {#streamtext-full-stream-markers}

- **What changed:** the `StreamText` full stream keeps `reasoning-start` /
  `reasoning-end` by default and no longer carries the Go-only
  `stream-start` and `response-metadata` chunks. `OnChunk` still receives
  them.
- **Am I affected?** Full-stream consumers that switch on
  `"stream-start"` or `"response-metadata"`.
- **What to do:** read those chunks in `OnChunk`.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Truncated streams finish with interrupted {#truncated-streams-interrupted}

- **What changed:** a provider stream that ends before its finish event
  reports `types.FinishReasonInterrupted` instead of `FinishReasonOther`, as
  TS. The step runs no tools and does not continue an agent loop.
- **Am I affected?** Code that detects truncated streams by checking
  `FinishReasonOther`.
- **What to do:** check `types.FinishReasonInterrupted`.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### A caller cancellation is not a TimeoutError {#caller-cancel-not-timeout}

- **What changed:** with `Timeout.Total` or `Timeout.PerStep` set,
  cancelling the context passed to `GenerateText` or `StreamText` came back
  wrapped in a `*ai.TimeoutError`. It now comes back as the cancellation
  error (`context.Canceled`), as TS rejects with the abort reason. A total
  deadline that fires during a step reports `Reason: total`, not `step`.
- **Am I affected?** `errors.As(err, &timeoutErr)` used to detect a caller
  cancel, or checks on `te.Reason == step` for a total deadline.
- **What to do:** check for the cancellation before the timeout:

  ```go
  var te *ai.TimeoutError
  switch {
  case errors.Is(err, context.Canceled):
  	// the caller cancelled (v0.5: a TimeoutError)
  case errors.As(err, &te):
  	// one of the SDK's own deadlines: te.Reason is total, step or chunk
  }
  ```

- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### A cancel during tool-call repair returns the abort reason {#repair-abort-reason}

- **What changed:** when `GenerateText` is cancelled or times out while
  `RepairToolCall` runs, it returns the context's cancel cause
  (`context.Cause`), or a `*ai.TimeoutError` with reason `step` / `total`,
  instead of a bare `context.Canceled` / `context.DeadlineExceeded`. TS
  rethrows the abort signal's reason. A plain `cancel()` still returns
  `context.Canceled` (review round 72). `ToolLoopAgent.GenerateAgent` and `Execute` report the same cause, wrapped as `step N failed: <cause>` (`errors.Is(err, cause)` holds; review round 80).
- **Am I affected?** Code that cancels with `context.WithCancelCause` or uses
  `Timeout.PerStep` / `Timeout.Total` together with `RepairToolCall`, and
  compares the error with `context.Canceled` or `context.DeadlineExceeded`.
- **What to do:** no action unless you match those sentinels; use
  `errors.Is(err, myCause)` or `errors.As(err, &timeoutErr)`.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Cancellations with a cause return the cause {#abort-cause-r94}

- **What changed:** A context cancelled with `context.WithCancelCause` made `ExperimentalStartBatch`, the tool-input refinement of `GenerateText` (`ExperimentalRefineToolInput`), queued tool calls that were not started, a `WorkflowAgent` call cancelled before it starts, a `StreamText` run cancelled at any point (while the model stream is read, while a tool input callback or its tools run, during a step's end callbacks, or before its first model call opens; `Err()`, `ReadAll()` and `OutputErr()`; queued steering messages then end with `aborted`; the full stream still ends with the context error after its `abort` part), and the realtime and live WebSocket streams return `context.Canceled`. v0.6 returns the cause (`context.Cause(ctx)`), as TS `abortSignal.throwIfAborted()` throws `signal.reason`. A plain `cancel()` still returns `context.Canceled`, and a timeout keeps its `*ai.TimeoutError` (review rounds 94, 95).
  Round 95 adds: with `ToolCallConcurrency` below the number of tool calls, an abort while the last calls run fails the step with the abort cause (TS mapWithConcurrency), also in a `WorkflowAgent` resume of approved calls. The ToolLoopAgent legacy loop returns the abort cause and, for a step timeout during tools, the step `*ai.TimeoutError` (still `errors.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded)`). Luma image, Rev.ai and fal transcription polling, and the `GenerateVideo` status wait return the cause. The Gateway speech and transcription streams fail with the cause itself, not a `GatewayResponseError` that wraps it. A WebSocket stream cancelled before or during its handshake fails with the cause. The Vercel sandbox stdin writer, the MCP HTTP and SSE transports' `Send`, the ACP and Grok Build `PrepareSandbox` and the bash tool return the cause.
- **Am I affected?** Code that cancels with `context.WithCancelCause` and checks `errors.Is(err, context.Canceled)` on these calls.
- **What to do:** Check `errors.Is(err, cause)` for your own cause, or `ctx.Err()` for the cancellation itself.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### The per-step timeout starts before PrepareStep {#step-timeout-before-preparestep}

- **What changed:** `GenerateText` and `StreamText` start the
  `Timeout.PerStep` timer at the top of each step, as TS, so time spent in
  `PrepareStep` counts toward it.
- **Am I affected?** A slow `PrepareStep` together with `Timeout.PerStep`.
- **What to do:** size `PerStep` to include `PrepareStep`.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### MaxTokens below 1 is rejected {#max-tokens-min}

- **What changed:** as TS `prepareLanguageModelCallOptions`, `GenerateText`,
  `StreamText`, `GenerateObject`, `StreamObject`, batch text requests,
  `PrepareStep` overrides and the legacy `ToolLoopAgent` fail with
  `*errors.InvalidArgumentError` ("maxOutputTokens must be >= 1") when
  `MaxTokens` is 0 or negative. v0.5 sent the value to the provider.
  `WorkflowAgent.Stream` still sends it unchecked, as TS `doStreamStep`
  (review round 87e).
- **Am I affected?** `MaxTokens:` pointing to 0 or a negative value.
- **What to do:** leave `MaxTokens` nil for the provider default, or set at
  least 1:

  ```go
  maxTokens := 1024
  opts := ai.GenerateTextOptions{MaxTokens: &maxTokens}
  _ = opts
  ```

- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Start events report a Prompt string as a user message {#start-event-messages}

- **What changed:** as in TS (`initialPrompt.messages`), the
  `OnStartEvent.Messages` of `GenerateText` and `StreamText` are the
  standardized messages. With `Prompt: "hi"` they hold one user message with
  the text "hi". In v0.5 they were empty for a `Prompt` call.
- **Am I affected?** `OnStart:` callbacks that read `Messages` and expect it
  empty for `Prompt` calls.
- **What to do:** read `OnStartEvent.Prompt` for the original string.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Deprecated tool execution callbacks run only when the new one is unset {#tool-execution-callback-aliases}

- **What changed:** `GenerateText` and `StreamText` called both
  `OnToolExecutionStart` and its deprecated alias `OnToolCallStart` (and
  both `OnToolExecutionEnd` and `OnToolCallFinish`) when both were set. They
  now call only the new callback, and the deprecated one only when the new
  one is nil, as TS `onToolExecutionStart ?? experimental_onToolCallStart`
  (review round 70).
- **Am I affected?** `GenerateTextOptions` or `StreamTextOptions` literals
