  `GenerateResult.Text`, so `GenerateText` content listed it after every
  reasoning part. v0.6 also adds one `types.TextContent` per non-empty text
  item to `GenerateResult.Content`, in wire order with the reasoning parts,
  as TS `doGenerate` pushes each item as its own part. `Text` is still the
  concatenation (review round 79).
- **Am I affected?** Code that reads `cohere` `DoGenerate` results'
  `Content` and expects no `types.TextContent`, or that relies on the text
  following all reasoning in `GenerateText` content.
- **What to do:** no action unless you range over `Content` from a Cohere
  model and skip or count parts by type.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

#### Cohere stream chunks are parsed like TS {#cohere-stream-chunk-parsing}

- **What changed:** v0.5 turned a Cohere stream chunk with a fractional
  `index` or token count into an error part (finish reason `error`) and
  failed `DoGenerate` on a fractional citation offset or token count; it
  emitted `ChunkTypeResponseMetadata` on `message-start` only when the frame
  had an `id`; and a `content-delta` with both `text` and `thinking` became
  reasoning. v0.6 follows TS `cohereChatChunkSchema`: numbers may be
  fractional (part ids are the JS text, `"0.5"`; usage keeps the integer
  part), every `message-start` emits response metadata, and a string `text`
  wins over `thinking` (review round 79). The chunk schema is checked before `index` is read, so a non-number `index` on a member that does not declare one (`message-start`, `citation-*`, `message-end`, tool members) is ignored instead of becoming an error part (review round 80).
- **Am I affected?** Raw `cohere` `DoStream` consumers that count
  `ChunkTypeResponseMetadata` parts or match error parts for fractional
  numbers.
- **What to do:** no action unless you consume Cohere `DoStream` parts
  directly.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

#### Cohere sends empty stop sequences {#cohere-empty-stop-sequences}

- **What changed:** v0.5 left `stop_sequences` out for
  `StopSequences: []string{}`; v0.6 sends `"stop_sequences": []`, as TS
  `stop_sequences: stopSequences` (see also
  [Empty stop sequences are sent](#empty-stop-sequences)). A nil slice is
  still omitted (review round 79).
- **Am I affected?** Cohere calls that pass a non-nil empty
  `StopSequences`.
- **What to do:** no action unless the API rejects the empty list; pass nil
  instead.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

#### A Cohere file part is an image only for the image top-level media type {#cohere-image-top-level-media-type}

- **What changed:** v0.5 sent a Cohere file part as an image when its
  media type started with `image` (`strings.HasPrefix`), for example
  `imagery/plain`. v0.6 compares the top-level media type (the text before
  the first `/`), as TS `getTopLevelMediaType(mediaType) === 'image'`
  (review round 80).
- **Am I affected?** Cohere prompts with file parts of such media types.
- **What to do:** no action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

#### Cohere decodes base64 document data {#cohere-base64-document-data}

- **What changed:** A string `FileData.DataString` document was sent as its base64 text; it is now base64-decoded and TextDecoder-decoded (TS `7a93864932`).
- **Am I affected?** Cohere text documents passed as base64 strings.
- **What to do:** Pass plain text with `FileDataTypeText`.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Mistral

#### Mistral: an empty choices array is an error {#mistral-empty-choices-error}

- **What changed:** v0.5 returned an empty result (finish reason `other`)
  from `DoGenerate` and skipped a stream chunk when the chat response had
  `choices: []`. v0.6 follows TS, which reads `choices[0]` without a guard:
  `DoGenerate` returns the error
  `Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'message')`, and the stream
  ends with the error
  `Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'delta')` (no error part, no
  finish part; parts the chunk already queued come first) (review round 80).
- **Am I affected?** `mistral` chat `DoGenerate` / `DoStream` callers whose
  endpoint (a proxy or a compatible server) can answer with
  `"choices": []`.
- **What to do:** no action unless you relied on the empty result; treat the
  error as a malformed response.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

#### Mistral conversation usage defaults to zero {#mistral-conversation-usage-defaults}

- **What changed:** a Mistral conversation (web search) response with
  `usage: {}` reported unknown token counts; it now reports 0, and
  `Usage.Raw` carries `prompt_tokens`, `completion_tokens`, `total_tokens` =
  0 (TS `mistralConversationUsageSchema` defaults; review round 82).
- **Am I affected?** `Usage.InputTokens == nil` checks on the conversation
  model.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Open Responses

#### openresponses custom tool inputs keep HTML characters literal {#openresponses-custom-tool-input-literal}

- **What changed:** v0.5 openresponses `custom_tool_call` results escaped
  `<`, `>` and `&` in `ToolCall.RawArguments` (`<`, `>`,
  `&`). v0.6 keeps them literal, as TS `JSON.stringify(input)` (review
  round 79). Both are the same JSON string.
- **Am I affected?** Code that compares `ToolCall.RawArguments` or
  `ToolCallContent.Input` of openresponses custom tool calls as text.
- **What to do:** no action unless you compare the escaped text; compare the
  decoded value.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

#### Hugging Face detects image types with the TS signatures {#huggingface-image-signature-sniff}

- **What changed:** huggingface resolves a bare or wildcard image media type
  with the TS image signatures (`detectMediaType`), so TIFF, AVIF and HEIC
  data are detected and `image/x-icon` is no longer guessed; v0.5 used
  net/http content sniffing (review round 82).
- **Am I affected?** `huggingface` image parts whose `MediaType` is `image`
  or `image/*`.
- **What to do:** Give the full media type.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Wrapper providers

#### Wrapper providers read only their own API key {#wrapper-api-keys}

- **What changed:** Baseten, Cerebras, DeepInfra, Fireworks, Together,
  Vercel, Vertex MaaS and the Azure delegates no longer fall back to
  `OPENAI_API_KEY`, and Azure DeepSeek no longer falls back to
  `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`. A missing key fails with `LoadAPIKeyError`. v0.5.3
  already did this for Baseten, Cerebras, DeepInfra, Vercel and Vertex MaaS.
- **Am I affected?** Deployments that set only `OPENAI_API_KEY` (or
  `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`) for these providers.
- **What to do:** set the provider's own variable (for example
  `FIREWORKS_API_KEY`, `TOGETHER_API_KEY`, `CEREBRAS_API_KEY`) or pass
  `APIKey`.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### BFL, Luma and fal

#### Failed generated-media downloads return ProviderError {#media-download-errors}

- **What changed:** as in TS, a non-2xx response when the provider fetches
  the generated image or audio is a `*providererrors.ProviderError` whose
  message is the HTTP status text, returned unwrapped. v0.5 returned a
  wrapped `*providererrors.DownloadError`.
- **Am I affected?** `errors.As(err, &downloadErr)` on BFL, Luma or fal
  results.
- **What to do:** match `*providererrors.ProviderError` and check
  `StatusCode`:

  ```go
  var provErr *providererrors.ProviderError
  if errors.As(err, &provErr) && provErr.StatusCode == 404 {
  	// the generated file is gone
  }
  ```

- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### KlingAI

#### KlingAI VideoModel() accepts any model ID {#klingai-video-model}

- **What changed:** as in TS, an unknown or empty ID is reported by `DoStart`
  (and so `GenerateVideo`) as `*providererrors.NoSuchModelError`, not by
  `VideoModel()`.
- **Am I affected?** Code that validates model IDs through the `VideoModel()`
  error.
- **What to do:** check the error from the call too.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Perplexity

#### Perplexity embedding provider options are validated {#perplexity-embedding-options}

- **What changed:** as in TS, a `dimensions` that is not a positive integer,
  or an `encodingFormat` other than `base64_int8` or `base64_binary`, fails
  `DoEmbed` / `DoEmbedMany` with `*providererrors.InvalidArgumentError`. v0.5
  truncated a fractional `dimensions` and ignored or sent other values.
- **Am I affected?** Perplexity embedding provider options.
- **What to do:** pass a positive integer and a supported format.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

#### Perplexity stream ids format numbers like JavaScript {#perplexity-stream-ids-js-numbers}

- **What changed:** v0.5 Perplexity agent streams built text, reasoning and
  search-result source ids with Go's fixed-notation float text (`1e21` as
  `1000000000000000000000`, `-0` as `-0`). v0.6 uses JS `String(number)`, as
  TS `getTextId` / `reasoning-${sequence_number}` / `String(result.id)`:
  `1e+21`, `0`, `1e-7` (review round 79). Integer ids such as `0` or `12`
  are unchanged.
- **Am I affected?** Only with unusual numeric indices or ids (exponent
  range, `-0`) in `ChunkTypeTextStart` / `ChunkTypeReasoningStart` ids or
  source ids.
- **What to do:** no action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

#### A typed decision state is sent as the native value {#perplexity-decision-typed-state}

- **What changed:** a single JSON decision state part whose Go value is a
  typed map, struct, typed slice or `json.RawMessage` was sent as `[value]`;
  it is now sent as the native object or array, as TS (`typeof single ===
  'object'`) (review round 82).
- **Am I affected?** `provider.DecisionJSONPart(v)` alone in the state with
  such a `v`.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

#### The stream emits no source for finance results {#perplexity-stream-finance-no-source}

- **What changed:** the Perplexity stream no longer emits a url-less `source`
  part for a `finance_results` output item (TS reads sources only from
  `search_results` / `fetch_url_results`) (review round 82).
- **Am I affected?** Consumers counting stream sources.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

#### A decision file by URL, reference or text fails with the TS error {#perplexity-decision-legacy-file-kind}

- **What changed:** a decision file given by the legacy `FileContent.URL` /
  `Reference` / `Text` field fails with `Perplexity decision file input: url`
  (or `reference`, `text`) instead of the image media type error, as TS
  (review round 82).
- **Am I affected?** Matching the old error for such parts.
- **What to do:** Pass image bytes.
- **Kind:** error text only

#### An empty thoughtSignature is sent {#perplexity-empty-thought-signature}

- **What changed:** v0.5 dropped `providerOptions["perplexity"]
  ["thoughtSignature"] = ""` on replayed tool calls and tool results; v0.6
  sends `"thought_signature": ""` on the `function_call` and
  `function_call_output` items, as TS `getThoughtSignature` sends any string
  (review round 83).
- **Am I affected?** `ToolCallContent` / `ToolResultContent` with
  `ProviderOptions["perplexity"]["thoughtSignature"]` set to `""`.
- **What to do:** No action unless you relied on an empty signature being
  dropped; leave the key out instead.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

#### Perplexity reports search queries {#perplexity-search-queries}

- **What changed:** `providerMetadata.perplexity.searchQueries` (`PerplexityMetadata.SearchQueries`) carries the deduplicated search queries of `search_results` output items and `response.reasoning.search_queries` events, null when there are none (TS `c6bbe485d7`).
- **Am I affected?** Code that compares the whole Perplexity provider metadata.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### ElevenLabs, HeyGen and Topaz

#### ElevenLabs wav, eleven_v4 and the voice path {#elevenlabs-wav-and-v4-dialogue}

- **What changed:** `outputFormat: "wav"` is sent as `wav_44100` (TS `3e5a12334b`; ElevenLabs rejected `wav`); `eleven_v4` always uses Text to Dialogue and the voice id in the path is `encodeURIComponent`-encoded (TS `48ab95d62a`). New `withTimestamps` option.
- **Am I affected?** `elevenlabs` speech with `wav`, `eleven_v4` or voice ids with reserved characters.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

#### ElevenLabs error messages from detail bodies {#elevenlabs-error-detail-messages}

- **What changed:** Error messages are read from the `detail` error shapes ElevenLabs returns (TS `7062ab6818`); v0.5 gave the HTTP status text.
- **Am I affected?** Code matching ElevenLabs error text.
- **What to do:** Match the error type and status code.
- **Kind:** error text only

#### HeyGen accepts any positive integer duration {#heygen-duration-positive-integer}

- **What changed:** Durations over 15 seconds failed locally; any positive integer is now accepted and `promptEnhancement: "ultraquality"` is allowed (TS `6f41ccc1f8`). Invalid values fail with `HeyGen duration must be a positive integer number of seconds.`.
- **Am I affected?** `heygen` video calls with `Duration` > 15, or code matching the old duration error.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

#### Topaz: redirected video uploads fail {#topaz-upload-redirect}

- **What changed:** The input-video upload no longer follows redirects (TS `c66402d340`, `redirect: 'error'`): a 3xx fails `DoStart` and the request is canceled. v0.5 followed it and net/http resent the whole video body to the redirect target.
- **Am I affected?** `topaz` video models with input videos whose upload URL redirects.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### QuiverAI

#### QuiverAI rounds the reasoning level to a supported effort {#quiverai-reasoning-rounding}

- **What changed:** v0.5 left `Reasoning: types.ReasoningNone` out of
  QuiverAI language-model requests with an `unsupported` warning whose
  feature is `reasoning effort none`, and sent `types.ReasoningMax` as
  `reasoning.effort: "max"`, which is not one of QuiverAI's efforts (`low`,
  `medium`, `high`, `xhigh`). v0.6 maps the level as TS `61a315e333`
  (`mapReasoningToProviderEffort`): `none` is sent as `low` and `max` as
  `xhigh`, each with a `compatibility` warning such as `reasoning "none" is
  not directly supported by this model. mapped to effort "low".`. An explicit
  `providerOptions.quiverai.reasoningEffort` still wins and is sent unchanged.
- **Am I affected?** `quiverai` language models (`ModelArrow2` and friends)
  called with `Reasoning` set to `types.ReasoningNone` or
  `types.ReasoningMax`, or code that matches the `reasoning effort none`
  warning feature.
- **What to do:** No action unless you relied on `none` leaving the effort to
  QuiverAI's default; leave `Reasoning` unset for that, or set
  `providerOptions.quiverai.reasoningEffort`.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Go-typed provider option values read like their JSON form {#provider-options-go-typed-lists}

- **What changed:** In v0.5 some provider options given as Go-typed values (`[]string`, `[][]interface{}`, `[]map[string]interface{}`, `map[string]bool`) were rejected or dropped where the JSON form (`[]interface{}`, `map[string]interface{}`) worked. In v0.6 they read like their JSON form, as TS parses the JSON value (review round 86): Azure transcription `locales`, `phraseList.phrases` and `diarization`, Moonshot system-message `tools`, Google Interactions `environment` (`sources`, `network.allowlist`) and Google embedding `content` (the call used to fail); OpenAI / Azure streaming transcription `streaming.include`, TS-shaped `openai.shell` environment `skills` and `networkPolicy.domainSecrets` (and a `types.ProviderReference` skill reference), Black Forest Labs video `keyframes` and Hume `context.utterances` (dropped or reshaped); replayed OpenAI computer-call `actions` and `pendingSafetyChecks` (sent empty).
- **Am I affected?** These options given as typed Go values: `providerOptions["azure"]["locales"|"phraseList"|"diarization"]`, `["moonshotai"]["tools"]`, `["google"]["environment"|"content"]`, `["openai"]["streaming"]["include"]`, `openai.shell` `ProviderArgs["environment"]`, computer `ToolCall.Arguments`, `["blackForestLabs"]["keyframes"]`, `["hume"]["context"]`.
- **What to do:** No action: calls that used to fail now succeed, and dropped values are sent.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Prodia: multipart responses parsed like TS {#prodia-multipart-r86}

- **What changed:** Prodia job responses are split with the TS `parseMultipart` rules: a response without a `Content-Type` boundary fails with `Prodia response missing multipart boundary in content-type: ` (the body was sniffed before), a quoted boundary is used with its quotes, a part whose Content-Disposition contains `name="job"` (also `filename="job"`) is the job part, an output part's media type is its Content-Type as sent (parameters kept), and an empty output part counts as the output (review round 86).
- **Am I affected?** Only with a proxy or server that sends unusual multipart output to `prodia` models.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Response headers read like TS fetch headers.get {#fetch-header-text}

- **What changed:** v0.5 read response headers with `Header.Get` (first line, raw bytes) and ported TS header regexes with RE2 `\s`. v0.6 reads them like TS fetch `headers.get`: repeated lines joined with ", " and each byte one character (Latin-1), with JS `\s` / `.` in the regexes. This covers `Response.Headers` / `ResponseMetadata.Headers` (`providerutils.ExtractHeaders`), download redirects (the next hop is `new URL(location, current)`) and the reported download content type, toolwebfetch (charset, Location, `cf-mitigated`, `DocumentOutput.ContentType`), MCP (WWW-Authenticate, OAuth discovery redirects, webhook signature headers, `mcp-session-id`, content type), the Prodia multipart boundary, the Google files upload URL, Bedrock request id and date, Moonshot cache-write token headers, the xAI speech trace id, OpenAI / xAI file download media types, ElevenLabs timestamps, Deepgram `dg-*` metadata and the workflow run-id / tail-index headers (review round 86b).
- **Am I affected?** Only for servers that repeat these headers or put non-ASCII or 0xA0 bytes in them; code reading non-ASCII values from `Response.Headers`.
- **What to do:** No action unless you relied on the first value of a repeated header or on raw UTF-8 header bytes.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Provider trims, regexps and URL escaping follow JavaScript {#r86b-js-whitespace-and-url-escaping}

- **What changed:** Trims, regexps and URL escaping now follow JavaScript: `String.prototype.trim` (U+FEFF trimmed, U+0085 kept) for Anthropic `anthropic-beta` header values, Gateway subprotocols and the credits team, Cohere streamed tool arguments, ElevenLabs transcripts and media types, and Google duration offsets (`Number.parseFloat`); Anthropic, Gateway and Google batch result rows are skipped only when JS-blank and parsed as read; `providerOptions.anthropic.anthropicBeta` entries are sent as given (no trim, `""` kept); Gateway credits / spend report, Google Live credential queries (a base URL's own query keeps its order) and the Cartesia STT URLs use `URLSearchParams` / `encodeURIComponent` escaping; the Gateway batch results error URL names the resolved base URL (review round 86b).
- **Am I affected?** Only with exotic input: Unicode spaces or U+FEFF around header values, tool arguments, batch rows or durations; `anthropicBeta` entries with spaces or `""`; team slugs, spend-report filters, generation ids, model ids or API keys holding `~ * ! ' ( )` or spaces.
- **What to do:** No action unless you rely on the old trimming or escaping.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Base URLs lose one trailing slash {#base-url-one-trailing-slash}

- **What changed:** TS `withoutTrailingSlash` removes ONE trailing `/`; Go removed all of them. OpenAI, xAI, Azure (`BaseURL`, `SpeechBaseURL`, `MaiBaseURL`), Gateway, Perplexity, HeyGen, Google Live paths and TypeSafe AI keep one, and the ByteDance, KlingAI, Replicate and BFL poll URLs no longer trim the normalized base again: `https://h/v1//` requests `https://h/v1//chat/completions` (review round 86b).
- **Am I affected?** Base URLs ending in `//`.
- **What to do:** Remove the extra slash.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### OpenAI realtime bearer token parsing follows the TS regexp {#openai-realtime-bearer-whitespace}

- **What changed:** The OpenAI realtime WebSocket (streaming transcription, speech translation) took the token from `Authorization: Bearer<ASCII space>...` only; v0.6 also accepts the separators JS `\s` matches (U+00A0, U+2003, U+FEFF, `\v`, ...) and rejects a token containing `\r` or U+2028 / U+2029, like TS `/^bearer\s+(.+)$/i` (review round 86b).
- **Am I affected?** Custom `Authorization` headers with non-ASCII spaces.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### OpenAI-compatible provider options names are JS-trimmed {#openai-compatible-provider-options-name-trim}

- **What changed:** The providerOptions key of an OpenAI-compatible provider (`Config.Name` up to the first `.`) is trimmed with JS trim (U+FEFF trimmed, U+0085 not); embedding option resolution and validation and the file-reference provider now trim too (they did not) (review round 86b).
- **Am I affected?** `Config{OpenAICompatible: true, Name: ...}` with surrounding whitespace in the name.
- **What to do:** No action unless your name has surrounding whitespace.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Batch result rows that are only JS whitespace are skipped {#jsonl-blank-rows}

- **What changed:** OpenAI, Anthropic, Gateway and Google batch result rows that are only JS whitespace (U+FEFF, U+3000 included) are skipped; a U+0085-only row or JSON behind U+00A0 is a parse error (TS `parseJsonLines`: `line.trim()` decides, the row is parsed as read) (review round 86b).
- **Am I affected?** No, unless the batch files contain such rows.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### xAI streaming STT URL query in TS order {#xai-stt-query-order}

- **What changed:** The xAI streaming STT WebSocket URL lists its query parameters in TS order with URLSearchParams escaping and JS number text (`vad_threshold=1e-7`, `~` as `%7E`) (review round 86b) A query in the base URL is re-parsed like URLSearchParams: an invalid percent escape stays literal (`%25`-encoded) instead of becoming an empty name or value (review round 87).
- **Am I affected?** Code matching the exact URL.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### A WebSocket text frame with invalid UTF-8 fails the connection {#websocket-invalid-utf8-text-frame}

- **What changed:** v0.5 decoded a WebSocket text frame that is not valid UTF-8 leniently (one U+FFFD per invalid subpart). Node's WebSocket (undici) fails the connection: v0.6 sends a close frame with status 1007 and reason `Received invalid UTF-8 in text frame.`, and `websocket.Receive` / `ReceiveLoop` return the new `websocket.ErrInvalidUTF8Text` (not a clean close), so the realtime, streaming transcription and speech streams fail like TS. Binary frames keep the TextDecoder replacement (review round 86b).
- **Am I affected?** Servers that send invalid UTF-8 in text frames to a `providerutils/websocket` connection.
- **What to do:** No action; match `errors.Is(err, websocket.ErrInvalidUTF8Text)` if you handle it.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Lowercasing and uppercasing follow JavaScript {#js-case-conversion}

- **What changed:** Where TS calls `toLowerCase()` / `toUpperCase()` on data (model IDs, media types, output formats, statuses, encoding names, env keys, file extensions, policy hosts, tool search text, telemetry provider names, the inferred stream error messages), Go now uses `providerutils.JSToLower` / `JSToUpper`: U+0130 lowercases to `i\u0307`, a word-final capital sigma to final sigma, U+00DF uppercases to `SS`, ligatures expand, and Unicode 17 case pairs Go's tables lack are mapped. The inferred stream provider error message is also trimmed with JS trim (review round 86b).
- **Am I affected?** Only for inputs with those characters.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### `Seed: 0` is sent to image and video models {#seed-zero-sent}

- **What changed:** v0.5 dropped `Seed: 0` in Bedrock image, Gateway image / video, Google and Vertex video, ByteDance, fal and Replicate video; v0.6 sends `seed: 0` (TS ef2dfb5e9f, `seed != null`). KlingAI warns that seed is unsupported for 0 too.
- **Am I affected?** `Seed:` set to a pointer to 0 in `ImageGenerateOptions` / `VideoModelV3CallOptions`.
- **What to do:** No action unless you relied on 0 meaning "no seed"; pass `nil` instead.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Google streams keep text and tool calls in part order {#google-stream-part-order}

- **What changed:** Google / Vertex streams emit function calls in part order: text before a call ends (`text-end`) before `tool-input-start`, text after it starts a new block; an empty text part with a `thoughtSignature` becomes its own empty block; replayed history keeps that part (TS fd7e8a75d7).
- **Am I affected?** Code that assumes one text block per Gemini step, or counts `ChunkTypeTextStart`.
- **What to do:** No action unless you merge text blocks by position; concatenate `ChunkTypeText` deltas across blocks.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Mistral Large 4 maps reasoning to low/medium/high {#mistral-large-4-reasoning}

- **What changed:** For `mistral-large-4` / `-4-0`, `Reasoning: none` sends `reasoning_effort: "low"` with a compatibility warning (was `none`, rejected by the API), `medium` is sent as `medium` (was `high`), and `providerOptions.mistral.reasoningEffort: "medium"` is accepted (TS 9ab42ea99a).
- **Am I affected?** `mistral.ModelMistralLarge4`, `ModelMistralLarge40` with `Reasoning`.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Open Responses streams report the server response {#openresponses-stream-response-metadata}

- **What changed:** v0.5 StreamText over an Open Responses (or QuiverAI) model reported the requested model ID and no response ID for the step; v0.6 reads them from the server's `response.created` event (`id`, `model`, `created_at`), like generate (TS 4b16915083).
- **Am I affected?** `openresponses.New`, `quiverai.New`, `StepResult.Response.ID`, `StepResult.Response.ModelID`, `Response.Timestamp` on streamed steps.
- **What to do:** No action unless you relied on the requested model ID or an empty response ID in streamed steps.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### A raw verbosity option reaches OpenAI-compatible hosts {#openai-compatible-raw-verbosity}

- **What changed:** A raw `verbosity` key in OpenAI-compatible provider options was dropped from the request; v0.6 sends it when `textVerbosity` is unset (`textVerbosity` still wins) (TS add01abd2c).
- **Am I affected?** `"verbosity"` inside `ProviderOptions["<provider>"]` / `["openaiCompatible"]` for together, gmicloud, ollama, fireworks, cerebras, deepinfra, baseten, Vertex xAI and other `openai.Config{OpenAICompatible: true}` hosts.
- **What to do:** No action unless you set a raw `verbosity` and rely on it not being sent; remove it or use `textVerbosity`.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Open Responses errors keep codes of any JSON type {#openresponses-error-fields}

- **What changed:** An Open Responses error with a numeric `code` (or `message`) failed the call or stream event as a decode error; v0.6 keeps it as the provider error (`Code` is JS `String(code)`). A `response.failed` with an empty `code` reports `""` as the raw finish reason, and a no-output response with an empty `incomplete_details.reason` reads "Responses API returned no output" (TS `??`) (review round 87).
- **Am I affected?** `openresponses.ResponseError`, `Config.GetResponseErrorMetadata`, `StreamProviderError` from Open Responses / QuiverAI streams, error text "Responses API returned no output".
- **What to do:** No action unless you match the old decode-error text.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Open Responses events and responses decode like TS {#openresponses-lenient-decode}

- **What changed:** v0.5 failed an Open Responses stream event (an error part) or the whole DoGenerate call when a field had an unexpected JSON type (for example a numeric `delta` or `status`, a string `sequence_number`). v0.6 decodes leniently like TS (`z.any()` events, an unvalidated generate body): string fields read as JS `String(value)`, `type` matches only as a string, other mismatched fields read as zero, and a non-object `item` / `error` / `response` is empty. An absent or null `delta` appended to accumulated tool arguments adds `"undefined"` / `"null"`, as JS string concatenation does in TS (review round 87b).
- **Am I affected?** `openresponses.StreamEvent`, `openresponses.OutputItem`, `openresponses.Usage` decoding; tests that expect an error part for a mistyped event.
- **What to do:** No action unless you relied on a mistyped event producing an error part.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### AI Gateway stream parts and responses decode like TS {#gateway-lenient-decode}

- **What changed:** v0.5 ended an AI Gateway stream with `failed to parse stream chunk: ...` when a stream part had a field of an unexpected JSON type (for example a numeric `id`), failed DoGenerate the same way, and dropped a generate content part with such a field. TS parses both with `z.any()` and passes the parts on, so v0.6 fails only on invalid JSON: a mistyped field reads as zero, a non-object part is skipped, and the string fields Go reads (ids, deltas, text, tool names and tool arguments) hold JS `String(value)` (TS 3fab50aa9b `gateway-language-model.ts`, review round 87b).
- **Am I affected?** `gateway` language models; code that matches `failed to parse stream chunk` for parts that are valid JSON.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Stream error codes follow TS presence {#stream-error-empty-code}

- **What changed:** The `StreamProviderError.Code` of a mid-stream error now follows TS: Moonshot and xAI Responses keep a present `code: ""` (v0.5 reported no code, TS `code ?? undefined`); Open Responses reports no code (nil) when the error has no `code` or `code: null` (v0.5 reported `""`, TS passes `error.code` as is) (review round 87b).
- **Am I affected?** `providererrors.StreamProviderError.Code` checks on moonshot, xAI Responses (`error`, `response.failed`) and Open Responses (`error`, `response.failed`) stream error chunks, for example `spe.Code == nil` or `spe.Code == ""`.
- **What to do:** No action unless you compare `Code` with `nil` or `""` for these providers.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Open Responses custom tools send an empty description {#openresponses-custom-tool-empty-description}

- **What changed:** A custom tool (`Config.CustomToolID`) whose `ProviderArgs["description"]` is `""` now sends `"description": ""` (TS `typeof description === 'string'`); v0.5 omitted it (review round 87b).
- **Am I affected?** `ProviderArgs` with `"description": ""` on an Open Responses custom tool.
- **What to do:** No action unless you set an empty description and the server rejects it.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Raw stream / stream_options provider options are sent in generate {#openai-compatible-raw-stream-options}

- **What changed:** Raw `stream` / `stream_options` keys in an OpenAI-compatible provider options map (`ProviderOptions["<name>"]`) are now sent in DoGenerate, like TS openai-compatible doGenerate, which spreads the raw options; DoStream still writes its own `stream: true` and `stream_options`. v0.5 dropped them in both (review round 87b).
- **Am I affected?** Provider options maps for together (`togetherai`), fireworks, gmicloud, ollama, Vertex xAI and `openai.Config{OpenAICompatible: true}` providers that contain `stream` or `stream_options`.
- **What to do:** No action unless you put `stream` / `stream_options` in provider options; remove them for generate calls.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### You.com 402 messages use any truthy body field {#youcom-402-message}

- **What changed:** The You.com 402 error message reads the body's `message`, `upgrade_url` and `reset_at` with JS `||` / truthiness and interpolates them with `String()`: a non-string value such as a number is used now; v0.5 used only non-empty strings and otherwise the default text (review round 87b).
- **Am I affected?** Error text from the `youcom` search / contents tools after an HTTP 402 whose body holds non-string `message`, `upgrade_url` or `reset_at` values.
- **What to do:** No action unless you match that error text.
- **Kind:** error text only

### AI Gateway credential and connection errors follow TS {#gateway-transport-errors}

- **What changed:** With no API key or OIDC token, v0.5 returned a connection error reading `Cannot connect to API: Post "<url>": AI Gateway authentication failed: ...`. v0.6 returns the `*gatewayerrors.GatewayAuthenticationError` itself, as TS does (its `asGatewayError` returns a gateway error as is). A failed connection now reads `Invalid error response format: Gateway request failed`, a retryable `GatewayResponseError` with status 500 and the transport error as its cause, like the TS APICallError "Cannot connect to API" path; v0.5 appended `: Cannot connect to API: Post "...": <dial error>`. An error from your own `Config.HTTPClient` Transport that is not a network error is treated like a TS fetch rejection: a gateway error comes back as is, and any other error gives `Invalid error response format: Gateway request failed: <message>` with that error as the cause (review round 87d).
- **Am I affected?** `gateway` models and provider calls. You are affected if your code matches the error text `Cannot connect to API` or `Gateway request failed: Cannot connect`, or type-asserts the top-level error of a missing-credential call.
- **What to do:** No action unless you match these error texts. Use `errors.As(err, &authErr)` with `*gatewayerrors.GatewayAuthenticationError` / `*gatewayerrors.GatewayResponseError`.
- **Kind:** error text only

### Provider credential, signing and key-function errors are returned unchanged {#provider-credential-errors-unchanged}

- **What changed:** An error raised while a provider authenticates a request now reaches the caller as the error itself, and the core retry does not retry it. This covers an Azure `ADTokenProvider`; a Google Vertex `AuthToken`, express-mode `APIKeyFunc`, MaaS, Vertex xAI or Vertex Anthropic token; an AWS SigV4 `CredentialProvider`, missing region or signing failure (Amazon Bedrock Mantle, Bedrock Anthropic, Anthropic on AWS); a Vertex project / location configuration error; and the `APIKeyFunc` of every provider that takes one. v0.5 returned a `*providererrors.ProviderError` (status 0) around `Cannot connect to API: Post "<url>": <error>` for the providers that authenticate in their HTTP transport, which the core retry treated as a connection failure (the token function ran up to three times), and a `*providererrors.ProviderError` around the error for a key function. TS throws these from the provider's fetch wrapper or from its headers resolution before the request, `handleFetchError` returns such a rejection unchanged, and only an APICallError with `isRetryable` is retried. The AWS credential-provider message now reads like TS: `AWS credential provider failed: <message>. Please ensure your credential provider returns valid AWS credentials with accessKeyId and secretAccessKey properties.` (Bedrock, Mantle and Bedrock Anthropic said `with AccessKeyID and SecretAccessKey fields`) (review round 87d).
- **Am I affected?** Code that handles a failing `ADTokenProvider`, `AuthToken`, `TokenSource`, `APIKeyFunc` or `CredentialProvider` through `*providererrors.ProviderError` (`errors.As(err, &providerErr)`, `providerErr.Message`), the text `Cannot connect to API` or `provider error (0)`, or the old AWS text `with AccessKeyID and SecretAccessKey fields`; code that counts on the retry calling a token function again.
- **What to do:** No action unless you match credential failures as a `ProviderError` or by that text. Match your own error with `errors.Is` / `errors.As`; it is returned as is. A token function that should be tried again must retry itself.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Decision models read typed Go criteria from direct DoDecide callers {#decision-questions-json}

- **What changed:** `DoDecide` of the OpenAI, Perplexity, Parallel, TypeSafe AI, Gateway and `ai.DecisionLanguageModel` decision models now converts typed Go containers in `DecisionQuestion.Instructions` / `Criteria` (`[]string`, `map[string]string`, ...) to their JSON form first (`provider.DecisionQuestionsJSON`), as `ExperimentalDecide` does; v0.5 silently dropped them when the model was called directly. TS providers always receive JSON (review round 87e).
- **Am I affected?** Code that calls `DoDecide` directly with typed criteria.
- **What to do:** No action; the typed criteria are now sent.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Google Interactions function_call without an id keeps the empty ID {#google-interactions-empty-call-id}

- **What changed:** v0.5.2 named a non-streaming Google Interactions `function_call` step without an `id` `call_N` (a per-response counter). v0.6 keeps the empty ID, as TS `parse-google-interactions-outputs.ts` (`toolCallId: call.id`). Built-in tool calls without an id still take a generated ID (TS `call.id || generateId()`; review round 87e).
- **Am I affected?** `InteractionsLanguageModel` results whose `ToolCalls[i].ID` / `ToolCallContent.ToolCallID` you read when the API omits the step `id`.
- **What to do:** No action unless you relied on a non-empty ID; match on `ToolName` and position.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Gemini usage after a continuation takes the later serviceTier and trafficType {#gemini-usage-merge-null}

- **What changed:** For a long-decode Gemini / Vertex response continued over several requests, the summed `usageMetadata` takes `serviceTier` / `trafficType` from the later request whenever it sent the key, an explicit `null` or `""` included (TS `addGoogleUsageMetadata` `{...a, ...b}`). `providerMetadata.google.serviceTier` reports `""` when the API sent `""` (TS `usageMetadata?.serviceTier ?? null`); v0.5 reported `null` (review round 87e).
- **Am I affected?** `ProviderMetadata["google"]["serviceTier"]`, `["usageMetadata"]` on google / googlevertex results.
- **What to do:** No action unless you treat `""` and `null` alike.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Google videoMetadata fps errors report inclusive false {#google-video-metadata-inclusive}

- **What changed:** An `fps` of 0 or less in `videoMetadata` reports `"inclusive": false` in the issue JSON, as zod v4's `gt(0)`; v0.5 wrote `true` (review round 87e).
- **Am I affected?** Code matching the text of the `ValidationError` from Google / Vertex file parts.
- **What to do:** No action unless you match that text.
- **Kind:** error text only

### OpenAI compaction parts carry only providerMetadata.openai {#openai-compaction-metadata-keys}

- **What changed:** An `openai.compaction` custom part (generate and stream, also `responses.CompactionEventToChunk`) carries `providerMetadata.openai = {type: "compaction", itemId, encryptedContent}` only, like TS; v0.5 also wrote `type`, `itemId` and `encryptedContent` at the top level of `ProviderMetadata`. Replaying a v0.5 part that has only the flat keys still works (review round 87e).
- **Am I affected?** Code that reads `CustomContent.ProviderMetadata` of `Kind == "openai.compaction"` parts at the top level (`meta["itemId"]`).
- **What to do:** Read the provider object:

  ```go
  var meta map[string]map[string]interface{}
  _ = json.Unmarshal(part.ProviderMetadata, &meta)
  itemID := meta["openai"]["itemId"]
  _ = itemID
  ```
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### OpenAI transcription sends an empty language or prompt option {#openai-transcription-empty-options}

- **What changed:** `ProviderOptions["openai"]["language"]: ""` and `["prompt"]: ""` are now sent as empty form fields on REST transcription, and `language: ""` is sent in the realtime transcription session, like TS (`value != null`); v0.5 omitted them. A present `openai.language` also wins over the Go-only `TranscriptionOptions.Language`. `TranscriptionStreamOpenAIOptionsValue` gains `HasLanguage` (review round 87e).
- **Am I affected?** OpenAI / Azure transcription calls whose provider options hold `"language": ""` or `"prompt": ""`.
- **What to do:** No action unless you pass empty strings there; remove the keys to send nothing.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### OpenAI computer calls keep an empty safety-check code or message {#openai-computer-safety-check-empty}

- **What changed:** The tool input of an OpenAI Responses `computer_call` keeps a pending safety check's `code` / `message` when the API sends `""` (TS `!= null`); v0.5 dropped it. `null` or absent is still dropped (review round 87e).
- **Am I affected?** `ToolCall.Arguments["pendingSafetyChecks"]` of the `computer` tool.
- **What to do:** No action unless you test for the key's presence.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Open Responses stream tool calls match item ids by JSON type {#openresponses-item-id-map-keys}

- **What changed:** Stream tool-call accumulation keys `item_id` like a TS `Map`: a numeric id and a string id with the same digits are different items, `null` and a missing id are their own keys, and an object id matches nothing. v0.5 compared the ids' string forms (review round 87e).
- **Am I affected?** Only Open Responses servers that send non-string `id` / `item_id` values.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Alibaba preserve_thinking spellings warn deprecated {#alibaba-preserve-thinking-deprecated}

- **What changed:** `providerOptions.alibaba.preserve_thinking` / `preserve-thinking` still work but now add a `deprecated` warning pointing to `preserveThinking`, like the other v0.5 Alibaba spellings (review round 87e).
- **Am I affected?** Alibaba calls using those spellings; code that expects no warnings.
- **What to do:** Use `preserveThinking`.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Open Responses replay sends an empty item id {#openresponses-replay-empty-item-id}

- **What changed:** A replayed reasoning, text, tool call or custom tool call whose provider options hold `itemId: ""` now sends `"id": ""`, and `reasoningEncryptedContent: ""` sends `"encrypted_content": ""`, like TS (`typeof itemId === 'string'`); v0.5 left them out. Reasoning parts merge only when both carry an id, and a text part with no `itemId` starts a different message from one with `itemId: ""` (review round 87e).
- **Am I affected?** Open Responses histories whose provider options hold `"itemId": ""` or `"reasoningEncryptedContent": ""`.
- **What to do:** No action unless you set those keys to `""`; remove them to send no id.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Open Responses provider metadata itemId keeps the item's id value {#openresponses-metadata-item-id}

- **What changed:** `providerMetadata[<provider>].itemId` on tool calls, text and reasoning is the item's `id` as sent: `""` is kept, a numeric id stays a number, and it is left out only when the item has no `id`; tool calls always carry the provider object. A stream tool call's `itemId` is the done item's, and a `""` `call_id` / `name` on `output_item.added` is kept (TS `??`; review round 87e).
- **Am I affected?** Code that reads `ProviderMetadata["open-responses"]["itemId"]` as a string or checks Open Responses tool call metadata for nil.
- **What to do:** No action unless you type-assert `itemId` to `string` or test the metadata for nil.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Live WebSocket close errors carry the close code and reason {#live-websocket-close-code}

- **What changed:** A Live or realtime socket that closes before finishing fails with `... WebSocket closed unexpectedly before finishing (code <N>, reason: <R>).` (`, reason: ...` only when the close frame has one; `1006` when the connection ended without a close frame), like TS. This covers OpenAI realtime translation, Google Live transcription and translation and Vertex Live transcription; v0.5 said `(code unknown)` (review round 87e).
- **Am I affected?** Code that matches `closed unexpectedly before finishing (code unknown)`.
- **What to do:** No action unless you match that text.
- **Kind:** error text only

### Provider responses keep a present empty string {#provider-response-empty-strings}

- **What changed:** Response fields TS checks with `!= null` keep a present `""`: Anthropic `providerMetadata.anthropic.stopSequence` (generate and stream); Gemini `finishMessage` and `continuationToken` (a `CONTINUATION` with an empty token still continues, sending it); Google Interactions `service_tier` (over the header), image `mime_type` (no `image/png` default) and a builtin tool result delta of `null` (the tool result is `null`). An `interaction.created` event with an empty id clears the interaction id in later provider metadata, while the stream still reconnects to and cancels the earlier id. v0.5 read `""` as absent (review round 87e).
- **Am I affected?** Code that reads those provider metadata keys or file media types and treats `""` like a missing value.
- **What to do:** No action unless you compare those values with `nil` / absence.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### OpenAI-style chat streams send text-start and text-end parts {#chat-stream-text-blocks}

- **What changed:** The raw `DoStream` of Groq, DeepSeek, Alibaba, OpenAI chat, the OpenAI-compatible model, the providers on `OpenAICompatStream` (Fireworks, Together, Ollama, Z.ai, GMI Cloud, Vertex xAI) and Mistral now wraps text in `ChunkTypeTextStart` / `ChunkTypeTextEnd` parts with TS's id (`txt-0`, or `0` for OpenAI, Alibaba and Mistral), and every text delta carries that id. The block ends where TS ends it (when reasoning starts for the compatible streams, Alibaba and Mistral; at the end before the tool calls). v0.5 sent text deltas without an id or block parts (review round 87e).
- **Am I affected?** Code that reads a provider's `DoStream` directly and counts parts or expects the first part after metadata to be text.
- **What to do:** Skip `ChunkTypeTextStart` / `ChunkTypeTextEnd`, or use them to group text.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Provider streams report response headers beside the stream {#stream-response-headers-capability}

- **What changed:** TS `doStream` returns `response.headers` beside the stream. The Go providers no longer emit a header-only `ChunkTypeResponseMetadata` part: `providerutils.WithResponseMetadata` (Anthropic, Gateway, Cohere, Moonshot, Open Responses), OpenAI chat and completion and `OpenAICompatStream` report the headers through the new optional `provider.StreamResponseHeaders` interface (`ResponseHeaders() map[string]string`), which StreamText and StreamObject read. A response-metadata part with headers still wins, for v0.5 providers (review round 87e).
- **Am I affected?** Code that reads a provider's `DoStream` directly and takes headers from a `ChunkTypeResponseMetadata` part, or counts those parts.
- **What to do:** Read the headers from the stream:

  ```go
  if h, ok := stream.(provider.StreamResponseHeaders); ok {
  	headers := h.ResponseHeaders()
  	_ = headers
  }
  ```
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Cerebras queueThreshold is a validated header {#cerebras-queue-threshold}

- **What changed:** `providerOptions.cerebras.queueThreshold` went into the request body as a raw key. v0.6 validates it (an integer from 50 to 20000, `InvalidArgumentError` otherwise) and sends it as the `queue_threshold` header; `providerMetadata.cerebras.serviceTier` reports the applied tier (TS 4a4e854fa6, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Cerebras calls with `queueThreshold`; consumers of `ProviderMetadata["cerebras"]`.
- **What to do:** No action unless you pass a value outside 50-20000.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Anthropic computer toolset member calls are checked {#anthropic-toolset-member-guard}

- **What changed:** A computer toolset member call's input `action` overrode the member the API selected, and unknown, disabled or undeclared members became tool calls. v0.6 makes the member the action (`action` comes last in the raw input unless the input has one), and unknown, disabled or undeclared members and invalid member input fail with `InvalidResponseDataError` (`Invalid toolset member input JSON.` / `expected an object.`). The local schema enum lists only enabled members (TS a161c0a665, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** `tools.ComputerToolset20260801` users; code that matches `InvalidResponseDataError`.
- **What to do:** No action unless you relied on the input's `action` or on calls to disabled members.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Vertex Anthropic, Bedrock Anthropic, Mantle and Anthropic AWS send credentials only to their endpoint {#anthropic-credential-redirects}

- **What changed:** The credential transports of Vertex Anthropic, Bedrock Anthropic, Bedrock Mantle and Anthropic on AWS ran on every redirect hop, so a cross-origin redirect received the token, API key, SigV4 signature and custom headers, and two of them modified the caller's request. v0.6 sends credentials and custom headers only to requests that are same-origin with the configured endpoint, and signs a clone (S2, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** A custom `BaseURL` for those providers that redirects to another origin.
- **What to do:** Point `BaseURL` at the final origin.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Claude on Vertex reads the legacy vertex options key {#anthropic-vertex-legacy-key}

- **What changed:** Claude on Vertex read only `providerOptions.googleVertex` (and `anthropic`). v0.6 also reads `providerOptions.vertex` when `googleVertex` is absent; only `googleVertex` counts as the custom key for metadata (TS 6400cf8b88, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Vertex Anthropic calls that put data under `ProviderOptions["vertex"]`.
- **What to do:** No action unless you put unrelated data under `vertex`.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Anthropic serviceTier follows the provider's allowed values {#anthropic-service-tier}

- **What changed:** `serviceTier` accepts `standard` and `priority` (MiniMax). A tier the provider does not take is omitted with an `unsupported` warning instead of failing validation (Anthropic: `auto`, `standard_only`; MiniMax: `standard`, `priority`); `providerMetadata.anthropic.serviceTier` reports the applied tier (`usage.service_tier`, else `service_tier`); an explicit null enum option (such as `serviceTier: nil`) now fails like TS (TS 8291ccf237, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Anthropic or MiniMax calls with `serviceTier`, or a nil enum option; readers of `providerMetadata.anthropic`.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Anthropic MCP connectors are disabled when a tool choice falls back {#anthropic-mcp-restricted-choice}

- **What changed:** MCP connectors stayed enabled when a named tool choice fell back to auto, or when the JSON response tool replaced a named or `none` choice. v0.6 sends `tool_configuration: {enabled: false}` for them in those cases (TS 5ab7ff14ad, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Anthropic calls with `MCPServers` and a named or `none` tool choice.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Anthropic ProviderError.Data holds the parsed error object {#anthropic-error-data}

- **What changed:** Anthropic `ProviderError.Data` holds the parsed error object `{type, error: {type, message, details?}}` (TS 6b101ac076). Error bodies with a `__proto__` key give the status text and no data (TS secureJsonParse; review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Code that reads `ProviderError.Data` from Anthropic errors.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Anthropic file upload metadata keys are present only when sent {#anthropic-files-v4}

- **What changed:** The upload result fills `ByteSize`, `CreatedAt` and `ExpiresAt`; metadata keys are omitted when the API omits them (v0.5 gave `""` / `0`); a response without filename, mime_type, size_bytes or created_at no longer fails. `FilesAPI` gains `GetFileMetadata`, `DownloadFile` and `DeleteFile` (TS 8b11ff6eb6, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Readers of `UploadFileResult.ProviderMetadata["anthropic"]`.
- **What to do:** Read the keys with a presence check.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Anthropic sends the structured-outputs beta for tools only with strict tool support {#anthropic-strict-tools-beta}

- **What changed:** `structured-outputs-2025-11-13` was sent for function tools whenever native structured output was on. v0.6 sends it only when strict tools are supported too (TS 4b8f00efff, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Anthropic-compatible platforms configured with `SupportsStrictTools: &false` and native output.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Anthropic batch results report toolset member calls as plain tool calls {#anthropic-batch-toolset-calls}

- **What changed:** A batch result mapped a toolset member call to the computer tool with the member as the action, which skipped the member checks. v0.6 reports it as a plain tool call named by the member, as TS `convertAnthropicBatchResponse` (review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Anthropic Message Batches with the computer toolset.
- **What to do:** Handle member names in batch results.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Vertex MaaS, OAuth, express and AI Gateway credentials stay on their origin {#vertex-maas-credentials-same-origin}

- **What changed:** The MaaS transport sent the Google token and custom headers to every URL, including cross-origin redirect hops (TS 977231e913); the Vertex OAuth, express `APIKeyFunc` and AI Gateway transports re-added credentials on such hops. v0.6 keeps them on the configured origin (S2, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** `googlevertex` MaaS, OAuth and express clients and `gateway` behind a redirect to another origin.
- **What to do:** No action unless you route these calls through another origin.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Cohere accepts untitled and tool citations; usage without tokens is unset {#cohere-partial-citations}

- **What changed:** Untitled-document or tool-output citations, or usage without `tokens`, failed with `Invalid JSON response`. v0.6 accepts them: tool-only citations are not sources, usage counts are nil when `usage.tokens` is missing, and citation metadata keeps the parsed sources (extra document keys, `tool_output`) without null fields (TS 3aad18a417, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Cohere chat with documents or tools; code that reads token counts or citation metadata.
- **What to do:** Check `Usage.InputTokens` / `OutputTokens` for nil.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Cohere streams emit source parts for citations {#cohere-stream-sources}

- **What changed:** Cohere streams ignored `citation-start` events. v0.6 emits `ChunkTypeSource` parts for them, and a malformed citation-start is an error part (TS 033a980fee, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Cohere streaming with documents; exhaustive switches over stream parts.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Cohere response ID is the v2 id {#cohere-response-id}

- **What changed:** `ResponseMetadata.ID` came from `generation_id`. v0.6 reads the v2 `id` and falls back to `generation_id` (TS 73400b299d, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Code that stores Cohere response ids.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Cohere streamed null tool arguments become {} {#cohere-null-tool-args}

- **What changed:** Streamed `null` tool arguments kept the text `null`. v0.6 gives `{}` (TS 27c44878c2, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Cohere streaming tool calls without arguments.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Mistral sends a named tool choice natively {#mistral-native-tool-choice}

- **What changed:** Mistral `ToolChoiceTool` filtered the tools to the named one and sent `any`. v0.6 sends every tool and `tool_choice: {type: "function", function: {name}}` (TS a7f8478781, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Mistral calls with a named tool choice.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Perplexity ToolChoiceNone sends no tools {#perplexity-tool-choice-none}

- **What changed:** With `ToolChoiceNone`, Perplexity still sent function and native MCP / connector tools, warned, and sent no `max_tool_calls`. v0.6 sends no tools and `max_tool_calls: 0` (overriding a caller value), with no tool warnings (TS 7772d0ca0f, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Perplexity calls with `ToolChoiceNone`.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Google ToolChoiceNone drops provider-defined tools {#google-tool-choice-none-provider-tools}

- **What changed:** With `ToolChoiceNone`, Google and Vertex still sent provider-defined tools (server-side execution), also in the JSON response tool fallback. v0.6 sends none and drops their `unsupported` warnings (TS 32dc1d2d17, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Google / Vertex calls with `ToolChoiceNone` and provider tools such as Google Search.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Gemini TTS sends the speech language {#gemini-tts-language}

- **What changed:** Gemini TTS dropped `Language` with an `unsupported` warning. v0.6 sends it as `speechConfig.languageCode` (except `auto`) (TS 3c6fb4322c, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Google speech calls that set `Language`.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Google Interactions sends labels {#google-interactions-labels}

- **What changed:** `providerOptions.google.labels` was ignored by the Interactions models. v0.6 sends it as top-level `labels` (an empty map as `{}`), and a non-string label fails (TS a210a29f9b, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Google / Vertex Interactions calls with labels.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Gemini streamed partial arguments with array gaps are skipped {#gemini-stream-array-gaps}

- **What changed:** Streamed `partialArgs` with an array index gap filled the holes, and an index above 65536 failed the stream. v0.6 skips such an argument, as TS (TS 26fc2a998f, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Gemini streaming tool calls with partial arguments.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Vertex Gemini transcription honors shared word timestamps {#vertex-gemini-transcription-words}

- **What changed:** The shared `TimestampGranularities: []string{"word"}` turns on `wordTimestamp` unless the provider option sets it, `Words` is filled, and segment, disabled and omitted word timings warn (TS 41465fd1b6, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Vertex Gemini transcription callers that set the new shared option.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Chat Completions refusals are returned as text {#openai-chat-refusals}

- **What changed:** v0.5 dropped `message.refusal` / `delta.refusal` (empty text, `stop`). v0.6 returns the refusal as text (a second text part after the content), and `stop` with a refusal is `content-filter`. This covers OpenAI and Azure chat, OpenAI compatible mode, Together, Fireworks, GMI Cloud, Z.ai, Ollama and Vertex xAI. Native OpenAI responses with a non-string `refusal` or malformed refusal logprobs now fail like TS (TS f6dbf04b5d, 646a7e3c2e, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** `FinishReasonStop` checks and `result.Text` on refused chat requests.
- **What to do:** No action unless you treat refusals as empty output.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Streamed chat logprobs hold every chunk {#openai-chat-logprobs-all-chunks}

- **What changed:** `providerMetadata.openai.logprobs` on a stream held the last chunk only. v0.6 holds every chunk, content then refusal logprobs, each `{token, logprob, top_logprobs}` (no `bytes`) (TS 646a7e3c2e, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Streaming chat calls that read logprobs.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Responses refusals are returned as text {#openai-responses-refusals}

- **What changed:** A Responses refusal part gave `""` text and `stop`, a non-streaming response with a refusal part failed with `Invalid JSON response`, and stream refusal events were dropped. v0.6 returns the refusal text with `content-filter` (unless a client tool call is present), includes it in `StructuredOutputText`, and validates the `response.refusal.*` events (TS 6b97088662, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** OpenAI / Azure Responses calls that the model refuses.
- **What to do:** No action unless you treat refusals as empty output.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### OpenAI-compatible inline text files are sent as text {#compat-inline-text-files}

- **What changed:** An OpenAI-compatible file part with inline text data and a `text/*` media type failed with a text-file-parts error. v0.6 sends it as a text part (TS 5f19ea5c61, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** OpenAI-compatible providers given inline text files.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### OpenAI chat sends message names from provider options {#openai-chat-message-names}

- **What changed:** `providerOptions.openai.name` is sent as `name` on system, developer, user and assistant messages; tool messages warn, and a non-string fails. `Message.Name` is now also sent on native system messages (TS 243bf221a8, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** OpenAI chat calls that set `Message.Name` or `providerOptions.openai.name`.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Azure merges azure and openai options {#azure-option-namespaces}

- **What changed:** Azure chat merges `azure` over `openai`. OpenAI-route transcription, speech and image apply OpenAI options given under `azure` (they were dropped or rejected by the strict schemas). `azure.language` on GPT-4o Transcribe or Whisper no longer warns; the Speech and MAI routes warn about OpenAI-only keys. An invalid azure option fails with `invalid azure provider options` (the field is in the cause), the TS `parseProviderOptions` text; v0.5 said `Invalid argument for parameter providerOptions.azure.<key>: ...` (TS 6400cf8b88, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Azure calls with options under `azure`; code that matches azure option error text.
- **What to do:** No action unless you match the old error text.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### OpenAI image edits send moderation in a fixed field order {#openai-image-edit-moderation}

- **What changed:** `/images/edits` did not send `moderation`, and the multipart fields followed Go map order. v0.6 sends it, in the TS field order (TS 5f9bb8eea4, 6549344e0a, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** OpenAI image edit calls.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### OpenAI MCP tool validation names tunnelId {#openai-mcp-tunnel-refine}

- **What changed:** "One of serverUrl or connectorId must be provided." became "One of serverUrl, connectorId, or tunnelId must be provided.", and `TunnelID` together with `ServerURL` or `ConnectorID` fails (TS 7a905226b6, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Code that matches the old MCP tool validation text.
- **What to do:** No action unless you match that text.
- **Kind:** error text only

### OpenAI transcription sends timestamp granularities only for verbose_json {#openai-transcription-granularities}

- **What changed:** `timestamp_granularities` is sent only for `verbose_json` (an explicit provider option with another format is not sent and warns). The shared `TranscriptionOptions.TimestampGranularities` is honored (provider option first, then the shared option, then `Timestamps`), and `Words` is set (TS 41465fd1b6, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** OpenAI and Azure transcription with `timestampGranularities`, `responseFormat` json / diarized_json, or `Timestamps: true` on non-whisper models.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### xAI video output upload URLs are validated {#xai-video-upload-url}

- **What changed:** `providerOptions.xai.output` was passed through raw. v0.6 reads `output.uploadUrl` (or the raw `upload_url`; `uploadUrl` wins), which must be an https URL without userinfo or fragment, or the call fails before the request with "Invalid xAI video provider options for upload delivery."; the result is an uploaded video with no URL, and API errors of an upload call carry no body or upload URL (TS 2e44144812, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** xAI video calls with `output`.
- **What to do:** Use an https upload URL without userinfo or fragment.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Black Forest Labs webhook image calls poll get_result {#bfl-webhook-get-result}

- **What changed:** Image calls with `webhookUrl` failed because the submit response has no `polling_url`. v0.6 polls `<baseURL>/get_result?id=`, treats a transient "Task not found" as pending (up to 20 times) and fails with "Black Forest Labs task <id> was not found via get_result before timing out" (TS eded4b8ed0, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** BFL image calls with `webhookUrl`.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Baseten DeepSeek V4 Pro JSON output uses an instruction {#baseten-deepseek-json}

- **What changed:** Structured output on `deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V4-Pro-0813` with reasoning returned empty content. v0.6 sends no `response_format`, prepends a JSON instruction system message and warns `compatibility` / `responseFormat` (default Model APIs path only; TS c4a8b80e02, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Baseten calls to that model with JSON output and reasoning.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Baseten chat uses ModelURL only for /sync/v1 {#baseten-chat-modelurl}

- **What changed:** Baseten chat used any `Config.ModelURL`. v0.6 uses it only when it contains `/sync/v1`, as TS `createChatModel`; any other `ModelURL` (a `/sync` embeddings deployment) leaves chat on `BaseURL`. v0.5.2 documented only `/sync/v1` for chat (review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** `baseten.Config{ModelURL: ...}` without `/sync/v1` used for chat models.
- **What to do:** Set `BaseURL` to that URL for chat instead of `ModelURL`.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Gladia polls for up to 10 minutes {#gladia-poll-timeout}

- **What changed:** Polling stopped after a fixed 60 s. v0.6 polls every second for up to 10 minutes, configurable with `Config.PollTimeout` / `PollInterval` or the per-call `pollTimeoutMs` / `pollIntervalMs`; the timeout reads "Transcription job polling timed out after <ms> ms", and invalid settings fail before the upload (TS 3ab5e75d79, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Gladia transcription callers; code that matches the old timeout text.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Gladia uses the v2 options {#gladia-v2-options}

- **What changed:** The v1 fields `contextPrompt`, `moderation`, `chapterization`, `nameConsistency`, `structuredDataExtraction(Config)`, `displayMode` and `diarizationConfig.enhanced` are no longer sent and warn `unsupported`; `language`, `detectLanguage`, `enableCodeSwitching`, `codeSwitchingConfig` and a `customVocabulary` array are translated to `language_config` / `custom_vocabulary_config` with `deprecated` warnings; requests carry `x-gladia-version`, and options are validated before the upload (TS c8875b71a3, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Gladia calls with v1 option keys.
- **What to do:** Move to `languageConfig` and `customVocabularyConfig`.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Gladia sends solaria model IDs {#gladia-model-id}

- **What changed:** Gladia never sent the model ID, and `Response.ModelID` was always `default`. v0.6 sends `solaria-*` IDs as `model`, omits `""` and `default`, warns `unsupported` / `modelId` for any other ID (which is still not sent), and reports the ID (TS c8875b71a3; see the known difference for other IDs; review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Gladia models created with an ID such as `whisper-v3`.
- **What to do:** Use `""`, `gladia.ModelSolaria1` or `gladia.ModelSolaria3`.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Gladia provider metadata carries the whole result {#gladia-provider-metadata-addons}

- **What changed:** `ProviderMetadata["gladia"]` carries `status`, `error_code` and the whole `result` object (every add-on and utterance key), and a failed job's error names the code ("... with error code N") (TS 9207c2b1e0, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Readers of Gladia provider metadata; code that matches failed-job text.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Deepgram speech rejects callback {#deepgram-speech-callback}

- **What changed:** `callback` made the model return the JSON acknowledgement as audio. v0.6 fails with `InvalidArgumentError` before any request; `callbackMethod` is ignored with a warning (TS 5cc6dcaa65, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Deepgram speech calls with `Callback`.
- **What to do:** Remove `Callback`.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Deepgram batch transcription warns about the streaming option {#deepgram-batch-streaming-warning}

- **What changed:** `DoTranscribe` warns `unsupported` / `providerOptions.deepgram.streaming`; live transcription is `DoStream` (TS 5b89b2560c, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Deepgram batch calls with streaming options.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Cartesia speech sends locale, normalization and generation options {#cartesia-speech-options}

- **What changed:** Cartesia speech sends `locale`, `normalization`, `pronunciation_dict_id`, `generation_config.volume` and `generation_config.emotion` when set, in the TS key order; a locale together with a provider language is an error (TS 392d2fa3a4, f10414f247, 47b666b6c7, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Cartesia speech calls with these options.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Cartesia streaming transcription sends keyterms and turn thresholds {#cartesia-ink2-keyterms}

- **What changed:** Cartesia streaming transcription sends `keyterm` and `turn_*` query parameters when set; thresholds without turn detection warn (TS 467b299146, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Cartesia streaming transcription with these options.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Replicate cancels abandoned predictions {#replicate-cancel}

- **What changed:** An aborted call or exhausted polling left the prediction running. v0.6 sends `POST /predictions/<id>/cancel` in the background (provider headers only, encoded id, 30 s bound), and a prediction without `id` is `Invalid JSON response` (TS 0d47a6cf8e, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Replicate image calls; stubbed Replicate servers.
- **What to do:** No action unless your stub rejects the cancel request.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### AssemblyAI flat error bodies give their message {#assemblyai-flat-error}

- **What changed:** AssemblyAI `{"error": "..."}` bodies gave the status text. v0.6 uses that message (TS c87b19ee75, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Code that matches AssemblyAI error text.
- **What to do:** No action unless you match that text.
- **Kind:** error text only

### Azure Entra tokens are not sent on cross-origin redirects {#azure-entra-token-redirects}

- **What changed:** The Azure Entra token transport added the `ADTokenProvider` token whenever `Authorization` was empty, which is what net/http leaves on a redirect hop to another origin, so the token reached that origin. v0.6 skips such hops (S2, review round 88).
- **Am I affected?** Azure endpoints that redirect to another origin and expect the Entra token there.
- **What to do:** No action unless your endpoint redirects to another origin.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Authorization is dropped on a redirect to another origin {#redirect-authorization-cross-origin}

- **What changed:** A provider request redirected to the same host on another port or scheme kept the `Authorization` header (net/http drops it only for another host). v0.6 drops it once the redirect chain crosses an origin (scheme, host and port) and does not add it back, as fetch does (TS postToApi / getFromApi). This covers the shared provider client and every credentialed request sent with its own `http.Client`: Bedrock (language, image, embedding, reranking), the OpenAI model catalog, Anthropic batch results, Azure speech / MAI image / speech transcription, Google files and batch uploads, Vertex transcription, Gateway realtime client secrets, OpenCode subscriptions, Vercel OIDC refresh, harness OAuth refresh, the workflow chat transports, the OPA policy client and the LangChain transport. Custom headers (`x-api-key` and the like) are still copied, as fetch copies them (review round 89). Round 90: a hop that comes back to the API origin after a foreign hop (A -> B -> A) gets no Authorization either (fetch never adds it back): the Anthropic AWS SigV4, Bedrock Mantle, Bedrock Anthropic, Vertex Anthropic and Vertex MaaS transports skip it, and the headers fetch keeps are still sent. Round 91: a cross-origin hop also drops `Proxy-Authorization` and `Cookie` (undici fetch), in the shared provider client and the MCP HTTP and Vercel Sandbox clients.
- **Am I affected?** A provider endpoint that redirects to another port or scheme and relies on the Bearer token (or a cookie) there.
- **What to do:** No action unless such a redirect target needs the token; point `BaseURL` at the final endpoint.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Anthropic schema constraint descriptions keep the schema's key order {#anthropic-constraint-key-order}

- **What changed:** When the Anthropic schema sanitizer (`toolutil.SanitizeAnthropicSchema`, used for Anthropic and Bedrock Anthropic tool and output schemas) moves an object-valued constraint such as `not` into the description, v0.5 wrote its JSON with sorted keys. v0.6 keeps the key order of the raw JSON schema, as TS `JSON.stringify` does (review round 89).
- **Am I affected?** Raw JSON schemas (`json.RawMessage`, `[]byte`, `*schema.OrderedSchema`, struct schemas) with `not` or another object-valued constraint, sent to Anthropic.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### GenerateResult.RawResponse is the raw response JSON (Gemini, Anthropic) {#raw-response-body}

- **What changed:** `GenerateResult.RawResponse` from google / googlevertex Gemini models was the typed `gemini.Response`, and from Anthropic models (also Bedrock Anthropic, Vertex Anthropic, Anthropic AWS, MiniMax) the internal typed response, so keys Go does not model were missing. v0.6 returns the parsed raw JSON (`map[string]interface{}`), as TS `response.body` is the raw value; every other provider already did (review round 89).
- **Am I affected?** Code that type-asserts `RawResponse.(gemini.Response)` or inspects Anthropic `RawResponse`.
- **What to do:** Decode the map, or `json.Marshal` it and unmarshal into your own type.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Groq API errors keep response headers, body and data {#groq-error-response-details}

- **What changed:** In v0.5, Groq API errors (chat and transcription) had empty `ResponseHeaders` / `ResponseBody`. In v0.6 they carry both; a schema-matching body sets `Data` to `{"error":{"message","type"}}`; a transcription error body outside the schema gives the HTTP status text instead of the raw body; retries honor `retry-after` / `retry-after-ms` (TS createJsonErrorResponseHandler) (review round 89).
- **Am I affected?** Code that reads `ProviderError.ResponseHeaders`, `.ResponseBody`, `.Data` or `.Message` from `groq` calls, or depends on Groq retry timing.
- **What to do:** No action unless you parsed the raw body out of `Message` for a Groq transcription error; read `ResponseBody` instead.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### HeyGen status polling validates redirects and keeps the API key on its origin {#heygen-status-redirects}

- **What changed:** In v0.5, HeyGen `VideoModel.DoStatus` followed any redirect and sent `x-api-key` to every hop. In v0.6, a hop to another origin is URL-validated (private targets fail with `DownloadError`) and carries no caller headers (TS getFromApi `validateUrl` / `credentialedOrigin`) (review round 89).
- **Am I affected?** A HeyGen-compatible proxy whose status endpoint redirects to another origin or a private address.
- **What to do:** Point `heygen.Config.BaseURL` at the final origin.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### HeyGen error data and response headers follow TS {#heygen-error-data-headers}

- **What changed:** `ProviderError.Data` from HeyGen is nil unless the body matches `{"error":{"message",...}}`, and then holds only `error.code` / `message` / `param`. `VideoModelV3ResponseInfo.Headers` joins repeated header values with `, ` (review round 89).
- **Am I affected?** Code that reads HeyGen `ProviderError.Data` or repeated response headers.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Perplexity tools without a description send no description key {#perplexity-tool-description}

- **What changed:** A Perplexity function tool with `Description: ""` sent `"description": ""`; v0.6 omits the key, as TS sends `tool.description` (review round 89).
- **Am I affected?** Perplexity tools without a description.
- **What to do:** No action.
- **Kind:** wire/protocol change

### Typed provider option structs are read {#typed-provider-options-structs}

- **What changed:** A provider's exported options struct (or a pointer to one) given as its `ProviderOptions` entry failed with `expected object` in providers that validate options with the shared parser, and `googlevertex.VertexEmbeddingProviderOptions` / `perplexity.PerplexityEmbeddingModelOptions` were silently ignored in v0.5.2. v0.6 reads such a struct through its JSON form (its json tags are the TS keys) and validates it like the TS options object (review round 89).
- **Am I affected?** Code that passes an options struct, for example `ProviderOptions: map[string]interface{}{"vertex": googlevertex.VertexEmbeddingProviderOptions{...}}`.
- **What to do:** No action; the options now take effect.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Replayed Google code execution parts are validated {#google-code-execution-replay}

- **What changed:** Replayed provider-executed `code_execution` calls and results with a missing or non-string `language` / `code` / `outcome` / `output`, or a non-JSON string input, now fail with a validation error, and extra keys are dropped (TS `codeExecutionInputSchema.parse` / `codeExecutionOutputSchema.parse`). v0.5 sent `""` for missing fields (review round 89).
- **Am I affected?** Google prompts that replay hand-built `code_execution` tool calls or results.
- **What to do:** Replay the parts the model returned.
- **Kind:** silent behavior change

### Groq usage no longer fills text / image input details {#groq-usage-input-details}
