- **ExtractJSONMiddleware without text-start**: `ExtractJSONMiddleware` extracts JSON from start-less text deltas and flushes at the end, as v0.5.2 (round 91). See
  [ExtractJSONMiddleware without text-start](./known-differences.mdx).
- **ExtractReasoning separator**: `ExtractReasoningOptions.Separator: ""` means the default (round 91). See
  [ExtractReasoning separator](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Embed and rerank end events of failed attempts**: Embed / EmbedMany / Rerank fire an end event with `Error` per failed attempt; TS leaks that span (round 91). See
  [Embed and rerank end events of failed attempts](./known-differences.mdx).
- **WorkflowChatTransport request headers**: `WorkflowChatTransport` keeps the v0.5.2 JSON content type and event-stream accept headers (round 91). See
  [WorkflowChatTransport request headers](./known-differences.mdx).
- **CreateIDGenerator empty fields**: `CreateIDGenerator` treats empty fields as unset (round 91). See
  [CreateIDGenerator empty fields](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Empty StopWhen slice**: An empty `StopWhen` slice is the default stop condition, not "no stop condition" (round 91). See
  [Empty StopWhen slice](./known-differences.mdx).
- **GenAI OpenTelemetry empty-string attributes**: GenAI OTel drops empty-string provider, response id and function id attributes (round 91). See
  [GenAI OpenTelemetry empty-string attributes](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Anthropic custom content parts**: Anthropic forwards the options of any caller-built custom part, as v0.5.2 (round 91). See
  [Anthropic custom content parts](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Chunk schema error text**: Chunk schema failures report compact Go issue text (round 91). See
  [Chunk schema error text](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Text and reasoning deltas of never-started blocks**: `StreamText` records deltas of blocks that never started (v0.5 providers send no block parts) (round 91). See
  [Text and reasoning deltas of never-started blocks](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Non-finite numbers in UI messages**: `ValidateUIMessages` reports any non-finite number as one `ValidationError` without a path (round 91). See
  [Non-finite numbers in UI messages](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Claude Code resume state from v0.5.2**: Claude Code resumes v0.5.2 token state by respawning the bridge; it never uses the token (round 91). See
  [Claude Code resume state from v0.5.2](./known-differences.mdx).
- **DownloadError cause text**: `DownloadError` writes the cause's message without the error name (round 91). See
  [DownloadError cause text](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Legacy V4 prompt telemetry details**: Legacy prompt telemetry serializes the normalized messages (tool-result file JSON, no empty system message) (round 91). See
  [Legacy V4 prompt telemetry details](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Bedrock Converse task budget**: Bedrock Converse forwards `anthropic.taskBudget`, as v0.5.2 (round 91). See
  [Bedrock Converse task budget](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Header-only API key authentication**: A credential header in `Config.Headers` skips the missing-key error, as v0.5.2 (round 91). See
  [Header-only API key authentication](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Vertex Anthropic labels header key order**: The Vertex labels header has sorted JSON keys (round 91). See
  [Vertex Anthropic labels header key order](./known-differences.mdx).
- **xAI cost metadata**: xAI keeps the v0.5.2 `providerMetadata.xai.cost` (round 91). See
  [xAI cost metadata](./known-differences.mdx).
- **xAI video response warnings**: xAI video status warnings become `provider-warning` warnings, as v0.5.2 (round 91). See
  [xAI video response warnings](./known-differences.mdx).
- **OpenAI-compatible namespace for provider options**: Z.AI, Fireworks and DeepSeek also read options from the `openai-compatible` namespace, as v0.5.2 (round 91). See
  [OpenAI-compatible namespace for provider options](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Ordered schemas inside provider options**: Schemas inside provider options are sent in Go map key order (round 91). See
  [Ordered schemas inside provider options](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Media request body passthrough keys**: Media request bodies follow TS order, passthrough keys sorted (round 91). See
  [Media request body passthrough keys](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Tencent SubAppId and serialization**: Tencent `SubAppID` is a decimal string; the API key is never serialized (round 91). See
  [Tencent SubAppId and serialization](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Deepgram error data request id**: Deepgram error data keeps the v0.5 `requestId` key (round 91). See
  [Deepgram error data request id](./known-differences.mdx).
- **LangGraph run stream invalid event data**: A LangGraph run stream event whose data is not JSON fails with a Go decode error text, not V8's SyntaxError (round 92). See
  [LangGraph run stream invalid event data](./known-differences.mdx).
- **UI message validation issue text**: `ValidateUIMessages` reports the first issue as `<path>: <message>`, not zod's issue list (round 92). See
  [UI message validation issue text](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Anthropic AWS settings checked at creation**: `anthropicaws.New` fails on a missing workspace ID or region, as v0.5.2 (round 92). See
  [Anthropic AWS settings checked at creation](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Open Responses accepts extra provider options**: The Open Responses model reads and sends the v0.5.2 options beyond `reasoningEffort` / `reasoningSummary` (round 92). See
  [Open Responses accepts extra provider options](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Realtime millisecond fields are integers**: Realtime event `StartMs` / `EndMs` / `OffsetMs` are v0.5.2 `*int` fields (round 92). See
  [Realtime millisecond fields are integers](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Empty API key settings**: An empty `Config.APIKey` or API key environment variable is the missing-key `LoadAPIKeyError` (round 92). See
  [Empty API key settings](./known-differences.mdx).
- **Infinity in provider values**: A provider number beyond the double range reads as ±Inf, and `encoding/json.Marshal` on it fails; use `jsonstr.Marshal` (round 96). See
  [Infinity in provider values](./known-differences.mdx).

## For coding agents {#for-coding-agents}

Follow these steps in order. Do each step completely before you start the
next one.

1. Upgrade the module and tidy:

   ```bash
   go get github.com/digitallysavvy/go-ai@v0.6.0
   go mod tidy
   ```

2. Build and vet. Fix every error with the matching entry under
   [Compile-time changes](#compile-time-changes):

   ```bash
   go build ./... && go vet ./...
   ```

3. Search the code for each pattern in the
   [quick-scan table](#quick-scan-table). This command covers most of
   them; run it from the module root:

   ```bash
   grep -rnE --include='*.go' \
     -e 'MaxRetries:|RecordInputs:|RecordOutputs:|Evaluate(OnEnd|End)Event\{|EvaluationModelCallEndEvent\{|TextContentBlock\{"' \
     -e '\.Text\(\)|OnFinish:|TotalTokens|ReasoningText|OnError:|PrepareStep:|OnLanguageModelCallStart:|Request\(\)' \
     -e 'stream-start|response-metadata|FinishReasonOther|TimeoutError|PerStep|MaxTokens:|OnStartEvent|StopWhen:|"call-"' \
     -e 'AllowSystemMessages|InstructionMessages|ToolCallConcurrency|ToolChoice|ProviderExecuted|ToolResults|ContextSchema:|ToolApproval:' \
     -e 'ToolCallID|ActiveTools:|Request\.Messages|DeadlineExceeded|context\.Canceled|ResponseMessages|LanguageModelCallOptions|WorkflowStreamOptions' \
     -e 'workflowAgent\.generate|WorkflowRunMultiplexer|OnChatSendMessage|UIMessageStreamWithOptions|ToUIMessageStream|KeepAliveMs' \
     -e 'Integrations:|"ai-sdk"|"go-ai"|TracerName|EnrichSpan|onEvaluate|ai\.evaluat|err\.Error\(\)|\.Provider\(\)' \
     -e 'promptCacheOptions|OPENAI_API_KEY|DEEPSEEK_API_KEY|DownloadError|klingai|perplexity|Issuer|RequestTimeoutMS|TimeoutMS' \
     -e 'codemode\.|wazero|acp\.|ClientAppName|AgentSettings|"terminal"|fx\.Settings|policy\.|cohere' \
     -e 'OnStepStart|PrepareCall:|ParseCompleteOutput|StreamTranscri|no such (evaluation|decision)Model|finishReason|is not allowed' \
     -e 'ToUIMessageChunk|event\.Ranking|ToolErrorContent|IsError|RepairText|NewStreamTextResultFromParts|xai\.(MCPServer|FileSearch)' \
     -e 'ai\.ranking|SerializableToolDef|ToolApprovalResponseContent|ConsumeSSEStream|NoObjectGeneratedError|ProviderMetadata|ReasoningFile' \
     -e 'openaitool\.|anthropic/tools|gateway/tools|providers/xai|\.Title|tool_search|NewShellTool|ProviderArgs|Warnings' \
     -e 'ResolveSerializableTools|Bash202|Sandbox is not available|return [A-Za-z]*[Ee]rr(or)?(\(.*\))?, nil|OnInput(Start|Delta|Available)|logprobs' \
     -e 'InputSchemaInput|ExperimentalRefineToolInput|SupportsDeferredResults|UIMessageStreamResponseInit|reasoningEncryptedContent|budgetTokens|redactedData|ExperimentalSandbox|CustomData|Timestamp\.IsZero' \
     -e 'ToolCallRepairError|InvalidToolCall|ValidateUIMessages|UncheckedStrictToolCalls|serviceTier|safetyIdentifier|promptCacheKey|\[DONE\]|"data-' \
     -e 'EmbeddingUsage|DoEmbed|SubscribeDiagnostic|NewSimpleStructSchema|NewStructSchema|inputSchemaInput|InputSchema\(|gmicloud|ollama|mistral|no embedding|ToolUIPart\{' \
     -e 'InvalidToolInputError|SpanRecorder|Ended\(\)|groundingMetadata|safetyRatings|promptFeedback|urlContextMetadata|StopSequences|RawArguments|ResponseHeaders|FinishReasonInterrupted|Prompt\{|ToolChoice\{Type|InputTokens' \
     -e 'validate:"|ai\.usage\.tokens|Collect(ToolApproval|ToolResult)Continuations|isAborted|isCancelled|ReadUIMessages|ChunksContext|StreamTranslat|DataString|Request\.Body|RawRequest|RequestBody\(\)|alibaba|moonshot|CompletionModel|computer|thoughtSignature|cerebras|bedrock|googlevertex|closed unexpectedly' \
     -e 'MCPToolSchema|ToolSpec|InputSchema|unsupported part type|languageCode|inputTranscription|SourceText|mai-image|azure|prodia|toolwebfetch|ToolChoice\{|s3://|ParametersMap' \
     -e 'PerChunk|FirstChunk|Response\.Headers|ResponseHeadersMap|ai\.prompt\.(toolChoice|messages|tools)|JSONSchema\(\)|ResponseFormat\.Schema|jsonschema:"|Files\(\)|Skills\(\)|DecisionJSONPart|signature|zai|huggingface|openresponses|anthropic|perplexity' \
     -e 'quiverai|reasoning effort none' \
     -e 'readableSkillRoots|nativeToolName|ResolveHostToolCall|IsMcpToolCall|SandboxCreator|OnBootstrap|invoke_agent|structuredOutputMode|wav_44100|eleven_v4|searchQueries|reasoningEffort|reasoning_effort|input_required|PolicySandbox|SetNetworkPolicy|FixJSON|web_search_call|AI_NoSuchToolError|propertyNames|IsRealtimeTranscriptionModelID|SSEParser|ToolUIPart|ExperimentalDecide|ToolApproval|SmoothStream|thinkingBudget|disable_parallel_tool_use|GatewayTimeoutError|heygen|topaz|toolwebfetch' \
     -e 'ToolCallConcurrency|ToolResults|ChunkTypeToolResult|WorkflowChatTransport|locales|phraseList|diarization|keyframes|utterances|domainSecrets|pendingSafetyChecks|responseJsonSchema|ClientAppVersion|expiresAfter|blackForestLabs|hume|rawInput' \
     -e 'GetTextFromDataURL|ReasonSet|HasReason|HasName\(\)|ToolCall\.Name|Encoding: "|ErrInvalidUTF8Text|"file-data"|"image-url"|anthropicBeta|BaseURL:.*//"|DeprecationWarning' \
     -e 'OutputErr\(\)|OnStepEnd|ApprovalReason|StructuredOutputText|MessageMetadata|ParallelToolCallMetadata|TakoSearch|reasoning-0|CODEX_HOME|CreateFormFile|multipart|failed to download image|provider options|\.Data\b|ProviderUsage|referenceImages|fal_webhook|invalid UI message chunk|SpeechResult' \
     -e 'FileData\{Type: types\.FileDataTypeURL|remote file URL must|Invalid URL|allowed types are|ImageContent\{|ReasoningFileContent|AbortReason|ReadUIMessageStream|continuationToken|errorCode|InteractionsModel|ClientSecret|ConversationModel|NewJSONSchema\(|ResponseBody|xai\.VideoModel|Size:|AspectRatio' \
     -e 'LangSmithDeploymentTransport|LANGGRAPH_API_KEY|aiobj-|LegacyContentType|ExperimentalDownload|WithShellSandboxDescription|MintBridgeToken|BRIDGE_AUTH|bridge\.token|DecisionModel\(|LoadAPIKeyError|RequestCounts|ResponseHeaders\["[A-Z]|exception\.type|toolCall\.durationMs|TranscriptionJob|DoStartBatch|amazon-bedrock request failed|elicitation|MaxInputBytesPerCall|textFormat|ExperimentalToolCallers|MockTextStream|provider error \(0\)' .
   ```

4. For each match, open the entry the table links to. Apply **What to do**.
   If the entry says "No action unless you ...", check the condition and
   move on if it does not apply.
5. Check what the grep cannot find: telemetry dashboards and queries
   ([Telemetry and events](#telemetry-and-events)), persisted ACP or fx
   state ([ACP state](#acp-state)), environment variables for wrapper
   providers ([Wrapper API keys](#wrapper-api-keys)), OpenAI-compatible
   servers that reject `tool_choice` ([Unset ToolChoice](#tool-choice-auto)),
   and golden files with escaped JSON ([JSON escaping](#json-not-html-escaped)).
6. Run the tests with the race detector, since tools now run concurrently:

   ```bash
   go test -race ./...
   ```

7. Fix failing tests by the entry that covers them. Do not change an
   assertion to match new behavior unless an entry on this page describes
   that behavior.
